	local container = context.section:Container()
	if not container then return end
	local create = context.create
	local frame = create("Frame", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = env.UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = env.Enum.AutomaticSize.Y}, container)
	create("UIGridLayout", {CellSize = env.UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 76), CellPadding = env.UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8),
		SortOrder = env.Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder}, frame)
	local labels = {"Stars", "Shooting Stars", "Comets", "Constellations", "Runes", "Stardust"}
	local colors = {{{70, 90, 150}, {12, 16, 40}}, {{150, 200, 255}, {14, 20, 46}}, {{110, 210, 255}, {16, 22, 50}},
		{{150, 205, 255}, {14, 18, 44}}, {{130, 220, 255}, {18, 30, 54}}, {{255, 214, 120}, {40, 28, 20}}}
	local ok, atlas = pcall(env.getcustomasset, "PulseHub/sky_fx_v6.jpg")
	if not ok then atlas = nil end
	local tiles = {}
	for index, key in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do
		local button = create("TextButton", {Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, BorderSizePixel = 0, LayoutOrder = index,
			BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(1, 1, 1)}, frame)
		context.corner(button, 12)
		create("UIGradient", {Color = env.ColorSequence.new(env.Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(colors[index][1])),
			env.Color3.fromRGB(table.unpack(colors[index][2]))), Rotation = 35}, button)
		local picture = create("ImageLabel", {Name = "Photo", Size = env.UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			ScaleType = env.Enum.ScaleType.Stretch, Visible = atlas ~= nil, Image = atlas or "", ZIndex = 2}, button)
		context.corner(picture, 12)
		picture.ImageRectOffset = env.Vector2.new((index - 1) % 4 * 256, math.floor((index - 1) / 4) * 128)
		picture.ImageRectSize = env.Vector2.new(256, 128)
		local shade = create("Frame", {AnchorPoint = env.Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = env.UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -5),
			Size = env.UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 20), BackgroundColor3 = env.Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.4,
			BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 3}, button)
		context.corner(shade, 7)
		local label = context.nova.label({Parent = shade, Text = context.translation.tr(labels[index]), Font = context.nova.Font.Semi(), Size = 12,
			Pos = env.UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0), Box = env.UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0), Truncate = true, Color = "Text", Z = 4})
		context.translation.bind(label, labels[index])
		local stroke = create("UIStroke", {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.8, ApplyStrokeMode = env.Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border}, button)
		context.nova.Theme.bind(stroke, "Color", "Glass")
		local tile = {key = key, stroke = stroke, hover = false}
		table.insert(tiles, tile)
		context.connect(button.MouseButton1Click, function() SkyFx.setOn(state, key, not state.cfg[key].on); state.paint() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseEnter, function() tile.hover = true; state.paint() end)
		context.connect(button.MouseLeave, function() tile.hover = false; state.paint() end)
	end
	state.paint = function()
		for _, tile in ipairs(tiles) do
			local selected = state.cfg[tile.key].on
			context.nova.paint(tile.stroke, "Color", (selected or tile.hover) and "Accent" or "Glass", "Quick")
			tile.stroke.Transparency, tile.stroke.Thickness = selected and 0 or (tile.hover and 0.35 or 0.8), selected and 2 or 1
		end
	end
	state.paint()
end

function SkyFx.install(context)
	local env, section = context.environment, context.section
	local state = {context = context, cfg = SkyFx.defaults(env), palettes = SkyFx.palettes(env), controls = {}, effects = {}, failures = {},
		time = 0, environmentClock = 1, radius = 160, squash = 0}
	local function slider(text, group, key, min, max, suffix)
		section:Slider({Text = text, Min = min, Max = max, Decimals = 0, Suffix = suffix, Default = state.cfg[group][key],
			Callback = function(value) SkyFx.set(state, group, key, value) end})
	end
	local function dropdown(text, group, key, values)
		local options = {}
		for _, value in ipairs(values) do table.insert(options, {code = value, label = value}) end
		section:Dropdown({Text = text, Default = state.cfg[group][key], Options = options, Callback = function(value)
			if value then SkyFx.set(state, group, key, value) end
		end})
	end
	local function color(text, group, key)
		section:Colorpicker({Text = text, Default = state.cfg[group][key], Callback = function(value)
			if env.typeof(value) == "Color3" then SkyFx.set(state, group, key, value) end
		end})
	end
	local function toggle(text, key, children)
		state.controls[key] = section:Toggle({Text = text, Default = false, Settings = children, Callback = function(value)
			SkyFx.set(state, key, "on", value == true)
			if state.paint then state.paint() end
		end})
	end
	section:Sub("SkyFX")
	section:Columns()
	section:Group({Title = "Sky Effects", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	pcall(SkyFx.gallery, state)
	section:Group({Title = "Sky Settings", Side = "Left", Pin = true})
	slider("Effect brightness", "general", "bright", 30, 200, "%")
	section:Button({Text = "Turn all off", Callback = function()
		for _, name in ipairs(SKY_FX_ORDER) do SkyFx.setOn(state, name, false) end
		if state.paint then state.paint() end
	end})
	section:Group({Title = "Night Sky", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	toggle("Stars", "stars", function()
		slider("Star density", "stars", "density", 10, 100, "%")
		slider("Star size", "stars", "size", 50, 200, "%")
		slider("Twinkle", "stars", "twinkle", 0, 100, "%")
		dropdown("Star color", "stars", "tint", {"Natural", "Cold", "Warm", "Pastel", "Rainbow"})
	end)
	toggle("Shooting Stars", "meteors", function()
		slider("Meteor frequency", "meteors", "rate", 5, 100, "%")
		slider("Tail length", "meteors", "length", 40, 200, "%")
		color("Meteor color", "meteors", "color")
	end)
	toggle("Comets", "comets", function()
		slider("Comet frequency", "comets", "rate", 5, 100, "%")
		slider("Comet size", "comets", "size", 50, 200, "%")
		color("Comet color", "comets", "color")
	end)
	section:Group({Title = "Magic", Side = "Right", Pin = true})
	toggle("Constellations", "net", function()
		dropdown("Placement", "net", "place", {"Sky", "Around me"})
		slider("Node count", "net", "nodes", 20, 140)
		slider("Link distance", "net", "reach", 0, 100, "%")
		slider("Drift speed", "net", "speed", 0, 100, "%")
		color("Line color", "net", "color")
		for _, entry in ipairs({{"Energy pulses", "pulses", true}, {"Follow cursor", "cursor", true},
			{"Rainbow lines", "rainbow", false}, {"Star figures", "figures", true}}) do
			section:Toggle({Text = entry[1], Default = entry[3], Callback = function(value) SkyFx.set(state, "net", entry[2], value == true) end})
		end
	end)
	toggle("Runes", "runes", function()
		slider("Rune amount", "runes", "amount", 10, 100, "%")
		slider("Rune size", "runes", "size", 50, 200, "%")
		dropdown("Rune set", "runes", "set", {"Runes", "Glyphs"})
		color("Rune color", "runes", "color")
	end)
	toggle("Stardust", "dust", function()
		slider("Dust amount", "dust", "amount", 10, 100, "%")
		dropdown("Dust color", "dust", "palette", {"Gold", "Silver", "Pink", "Aqua", "Rainbow"})
	end)
	local api = {cfg = state.cfg, scene = function(names) SkyFx.scene(state, names) end, sceneEnd = function() SkyFx.sceneEnd(state) end,
		set = function(group, key, value) SkyFx.set(state, group, key, value) end}
	env.getgenv().__pulseSkyFx = api
	if state.paint then state.paint() end
	if context.runService.RenderStepped then context.connect(context.runService.RenderStepped, function(delta)
		local ok, message = pcall(SkyFx.step, state, delta)
		if not ok then env.warn("[MYTHIC HUB] sky fx step: " .. tostring(message)) end
	end) end
	return state, function()
		SkyFx.clear(state)
		if env.getgenv().__pulseSkyFx == api then env.getgenv().__pulseSkyFx = nil end
	end
end

local AvatarSkins = {}
do -- Keep module-private locals out of the main chunk's register budget.

local AVATAR_PROPERTIES = {"BodyTypeScale", "DepthScale", "HeadScale", "HeightScale", "ProportionScale", "WidthScale",
	"Face", "Head", "LeftArm", "LeftLeg", "RightArm", "RightLeg", "Torso", "GraphicTShirt", "Pants", "Shirt"}
local AVATAR_COLORS = {"HeadColor", "LeftArmColor", "LeftLegColor", "RightArmColor", "RightLegColor", "TorsoColor"}
local AVATAR_PARTS = {Head = "Head", DynamicHead = "Head", Torso = "Torso", LeftArm = "LeftArm", RightArm = "RightArm",
	LeftLeg = "LeftLeg", RightLeg = "RightLeg"}
local AVATAR_ACCESSORIES = {HairAccessory = true, FaceAccessory = true, NeckAccessory = true, ShouldersAccessory = true,
	FrontAccessory = true, BackAccessory = true, WaistAccessory = true}

function AvatarSkins.serialize(description)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local saved = {p = {}, c = {}, acc = {}}
		for _, key in AVATAR_PROPERTIES do saved.p[key] = description[key] end
		for _, key in AVATAR_COLORS do
			local color = description[key]
			saved.c[key] = {math.floor(color.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(color.B * 255 + 0.5)}
		end
		for _, accessory in description:GetAccessories(true) do
			table.insert(saved.acc, {t = accessory.AccessoryType.Name, id = accessory.AssetId, l = accessory.IsLayered == true,
				o = accessory.Order, pf = accessory.Puffiness})
		end
		return saved
	end)
	if ok then return result end
	return nil
end

function AvatarSkins.deserialize(environment, saved)
	if type(saved) ~= "table" then return nil end
	local ok, description = pcall(function()
		local result = environment.Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
		for _, key in AVATAR_PROPERTIES do
			local value = type(saved.p) == "table" and saved.p[key]
			if type(value) == "number" then pcall(function() result[key] = value end) end
		end
		for _, key in AVATAR_COLORS do
			local color = type(saved.c) == "table" and saved.c[key]
			if type(color) ~= "table" then continue end
			pcall(function() result[key] = environment.Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(color[1]) or 0, tonumber(color[2]) or 0, tonumber(color[3]) or 0) end)
		end
		local accessories = {}
		for _, item in type(saved.acc) == "table" and saved.acc or {} do
			local valid, kind = pcall(function() return environment.Enum.AccessoryType[tostring(item.t)] end)
			if not valid or not kind or not tonumber(item.id) then continue end
			table.insert(accessories, {AccessoryType = kind, AssetId = tonumber(item.id), IsLayered = item.l == true,
				Order = tonumber(item.o), Puffiness = tonumber(item.pf)})
		end
		result:SetAccessories(accessories, true)
		return result
	end)
	if ok then return description end
	return nil
end

function AvatarSkins.bundle(context, id)
	local ok, description, name = pcall(function()
		local bundle = context.assetService:GetBundleDetailsAsync(id)
		for _, item in bundle.Items or {} do
			if item.Type == "UserOutfit" then return context.players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromOutfitId(item.Id), bundle.Name end
		end
	end)
	if ok and description then return description, name end

	ok, description = pcall(context.avatar.GetItemDetails, context.avatar, id, context.environment.Enum.AvatarItemType.Bundle)
	if not ok or type(description) ~= "table" then return nil, "Bundle not found (rate limit?)" end
	local bundle = description
	local items = bundle.BundledItems or bundle.Items
	if type(items) ~= "table" or #items == 0 then return nil, "Bundle is empty" end
	description = context.environment.Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
	local accessories, parts = {}, 0
	for _, item in items do
		local kind = tostring(item.AssetType)
		local part = AVATAR_PARTS[kind]
		if part then
			pcall(function() description[part] = item.Id end)
			parts += 1
			continue
		end
		if not AVATAR_ACCESSORIES[kind] then continue end
		accessories[kind] = accessories[kind] or {}
		table.insert(accessories[kind], tostring(item.Id))
	end
	for kind, values in accessories do pcall(function() description[kind] = table.concat(values, ",") end) end
	if parts == 0 and not next(accessories) then return nil, "This bundle has no body parts" end
	return description, bundle.Name or "ID " .. tostring(id)
end

function AvatarSkins.user(context, value)
	local id = tonumber(value) or context.players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(value)
	local name = tostring(value)
	pcall(function() name = context.players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(id) end)
	return context.players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(id), name, id
end

function AvatarSkins.joints(model)
	local result = {}
	for _, joint in model:GetDescendants() do
		if joint:IsA("Motor6D") and joint.Part0 and joint.Part1 then
			result[joint.Part1.Name] = {joint = joint, parent = joint.Part0, part = joint.Part1, motor = true}
			continue
		end
		if not joint:IsA("AnimationConstraint") or not joint.Attachment0 or not joint.Attachment1 then continue end
		local first, second = joint.Attachment0, joint.Attachment1
		if first.Parent and second.Parent then result[second.Parent.Name] = {joint = joint, parent = first.Parent, part = second.Parent, a0 = first, a1 = second} end
	end
	return result
end

function AvatarSkins.frames(link)
	if link.motor then return link.joint.C0, link.joint.C1 end
	return link.a0.CFrame, link.a1.CFrame
end

function AvatarSkins.ground(environment, model, joints)
	local root, foot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), model:FindFirstChild("LeftFoot")
	if not root or not foot then return nil end
	local chain, part = {}, foot
	while part and part ~= root do
		local link = joints[part.Name]
		if not link or #chain >= 64 then return nil end
		table.insert(chain, 1, link)
		part = link.parent
	end
	local frame = environment.CFrame.new()
	for _, link in chain do
		local first, second = AvatarSkins.frames(link)
		frame = frame * first * second:Inverse()
	end
	return foot.Size.Y / 2 - frame.Position.Y
end

function AvatarSkins.ghost(part)
	part.CanCollide, part.CanTouch, part.CanQuery, part.Massless, part.CastShadow = false, false, false, true, true
	part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanCollide"):Connect(function() if part.CanCollide then part.CanCollide = false end end)
end

function AvatarSkins.prepare(state, model)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local parts = {}
	for _, object in model:GetDescendants() do
		if object:IsA("BaseScript") then object:Destroy(); continue end
		if object:IsA("BasePart") then
			AvatarSkins.ghost(object)
			if object.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then table.insert(parts, object) end
			continue
		end
		if object:IsA("AnimationConstraint") then object.IsKinematic = true end
		if object:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then object.Enabled = false end
	end
	local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if humanoid then pcall(function()
		humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = environment.Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
		humanoid.HealthDisplayType = environment.Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff
		humanoid.EvaluateStateMachine, humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false, false
	end) end
	local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if root then root.Anchored, root.Transparency = true, 1 end
	model.Name = "PulseSkin"
	model.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object) if object:IsA("BasePart") then AvatarSkins.ghost(object) end end)
	local joints = AvatarSkins.joints(model)
	return joints, AvatarSkins.ground(environment, model, joints), parts
end

function AvatarSkins.attach(state, character)
	state.character, state.links = character, nil
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local joints = AvatarSkins.joints(character)
	local ground = AvatarSkins.ground(environment, character, joints)
	if not ground or not state.ground or ground <= 0 then return false end
	local links, byPart = {}, {}
	for name, source in joints do
		local target = state.joints[name]
		if not target then continue end
		local source0, source1 = AvatarSkins.frames(source)
		local target0, target1 = AvatarSkins.frames(target)
		table.insert(links, {source = source, target = target, root = source.parent.Name == "HumanoidRootPart",
			restSource = source0 * source1:Inverse(), restTarget = target0 * target1:Inverse(), inverse = target0:Inverse(),
			target1 = target1, pivot0 = environment.CFrame.new(target0.Position), pivot1 = environment.CFrame.new(target1.Position):Inverse()})
		byPart[target.part.Name] = #links
	end
	if #links == 0 then return false end
	state.scale, state.lift = state.ground / ground, state.ground - ground
	state.chains, state.feet = {}, {}
	for name in byPart do
		local chain, part, depth = {}, name, 0
		while part and byPart[part] and depth < 32 do
			depth += 1
			local index = byPart[part]
			table.insert(chain, 1, index)
			local parent = links[index].target.parent
			part = parent.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and parent.Name or nil
			if not part then state.chains[name] = chain end
		end
	end
	for _, name in {"LeftFoot", "RightFoot"} do
		local source, target = character:FindFirstChild(name), state.model:FindFirstChild(name)
		if state.chains[name] and source and target and source:IsA("BasePart") and target:IsA("BasePart") then
			table.insert(state.feet, {chain = state.chains[name], source = source.Size.Y / 2, target = target.Size.Y / 2})
		end
	end
	local hand = state.model:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
	local attachment = hand and hand:FindFirstChild("RightGripAttachment")
	state.modelGrip = hand and environment.CFrame.new(0, -hand.Size.Y / 2, 0) or nil
	if attachment and attachment:IsA("Attachment") then state.modelGrip = attachment.CFrame end
	state.links = links
	AvatarSkins.notify(state)
	return true
end

function AvatarSkins.drive(state, character)
	local sourceRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local targetRoot = state.model and state.model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not state.links or not sourceRoot or not targetRoot then return false end
	local environment = state.context.environment
	for _, link in state.links do
		local first, second = AvatarSkins.frames(link.source)
		local relative = first * link.source.joint.Transform * second:Inverse()
		local target
		if link.root then
			target = environment.CFrame.new(link.restTarget.Position + (relative.Position - link.restSource.Position) * state.scale) * relative.Rotation
		else
			target = link.pivot0 * relative.Rotation * link.pivot1
		end
		link.relativeSource, link.relativeTarget = relative, target
		link.target.joint.Transform = link.inverse * target * link.target1
	end
	local lift = state.lift
	if #state.feet > 0 then
		local sourceLow, targetLow = math.huge, math.huge
		for _, foot in state.feet do
			local source, target = environment.CFrame.identity, environment.CFrame.identity
			for _, index in foot.chain do
				local link = state.links[index]
				source, target = source * link.relativeSource, target * link.relativeTarget
			end
			sourceLow, targetLow = math.min(sourceLow, source.Y - foot.source), math.min(targetLow, target.Y - foot.target)
		end
		if sourceLow < math.huge and targetLow < math.huge then lift = sourceLow - targetLow end
	end
	state.rootFrame, state.sourceFrame = sourceRoot.CFrame * environment.CFrame.new(0, lift, 0), sourceRoot.CFrame
	targetRoot.CFrame = state.rootFrame
	return true
end

function AvatarSkins.world(state, target, name)
	local chain = state.chains and state.chains[name]
	if not chain then return nil end
	local frame = target and state.rootFrame or state.sourceFrame
	for _, index in chain do
		local link = state.links[index]
		frame *= target and link.relativeTarget or link.relativeSource
	end
	return frame
end

function AvatarSkins.notify(state)
	local globals = state.context.environment.getgenv()
	local callback = rawget(globals, "__pulseSkinChanged")
	if type(callback) == "function" then state.context.environment.task.defer(function() pcall(callback, state.player) end) end
end

function AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state, except)
	for joint, saved in state.grips do
		if joint == except then continue end
		pcall(function() if joint.Parent and saved.applied and joint.C0:FuzzyEq(saved.applied, 0.001) then joint.C0 = saved.original end end)
		state.grips[joint] = nil
	end
end

function AvatarSkins.grip(state, character)
	local hand = character:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
	local joint = hand and hand:FindFirstChild("RightGrip")
	AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state, joint)
	if not joint or not joint:IsA("JointInstance") or not state.modelGrip then return end
	local saved = state.grips[joint]
	if not saved or not saved.applied or not joint.C0:FuzzyEq(saved.applied, 0.001) then
		saved = {original = joint.C0}
		state.grips[joint] = saved
	end
	local source, target = AvatarSkins.world(state, false, "RightHand"), AvatarSkins.world(state, true, "RightHand")
	if not source or not target then return end
	saved.applied = source:Inverse() * target * state.modelGrip
	joint.C0 = saved.applied
end

function AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(state)
	for object, saved in state.mounts do
		pcall(function()
			if object.Parent and saved.applied and object[saved.property]:FuzzyEq(saved.applied, 0.0001) then object[saved.property] = saved.original end
		end)
		state.mounts[object] = nil
	end
	state.mountList, state.mountCharacter = nil, nil
end

function AvatarSkins.scanMounts(state, character)
	local torso = {LowerTorso = character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso"), UpperTorso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")}
	local function partName(part)
		for name, value in torso do if value == part then return name end end
	end
	local mounts, seen = {}, {}
	local displays = state.context.workspace:FindFirstChild("WeaponDisplays")
	for _, display in displays and displays:GetChildren() or {} do
		local constraint = display:FindFirstChildOfClass("RigidConstraint")
		local attachment = constraint and constraint.Attachment0
		local name = attachment and partName(attachment.Parent)
		if name and not seen[attachment] then
			seen[attachment] = true
			table.insert(mounts, {object = attachment, property = "CFrame", part = name})
		end
		local joint = display:FindFirstChild("PulseMount")
		name = joint and joint:IsA("JointInstance") and partName(joint.Part0)
		if name and not seen[joint] then
			seen[joint] = true
			table.insert(mounts, {object = joint, property = "C0", part = name, anchor = joint:GetAttribute("PulseAnchor")})
		end
	end
	state.mountList, state.mountCharacter, state.mountAt = mounts, character, state.context.environment.os.clock()
end

function AvatarSkins.mountOriginal(state, object)
	local saved = state.mounts[object]
	if saved and object.Parent and saved.applied and object[saved.property]:FuzzyEq(saved.applied, 0.0001) then return saved.original end
	return nil
end

function AvatarSkins.scaleOffset(value, original, replacement)
	local absolute = math.abs(value)
	local inside = math.min(absolute, original)
	local scaled = (original > 0.001 and inside * replacement / original or 0) + math.max(absolute - original, 0)
	return value < 0 and -scaled or scaled
end

function AvatarSkins.followMounts(state, character)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	if state.mountCharacter ~= character or environment.os.clock() - (state.mountAt or 0) > 0.5 then AvatarSkins.scanMounts(state, character) end
	for _, mount in state.mountList or {} do
		local object = mount.object
		local originalPart = character:FindFirstChild(mount.part)
		local modelPart = state.model:FindFirstChild(mount.part)
		local source, target = AvatarSkins.world(state, false, mount.part), AvatarSkins.world(state, true, mount.part)
		if not object.Parent or not originalPart or not modelPart or not source or not target then continue end
		local current, saved = object[mount.property], state.mounts[object]
		if not saved or not saved.applied or not current:FuzzyEq(saved.applied, 0.0001) then
			saved = {original = current, property = mount.property}
			state.mounts[object] = saved
		end
		local position = saved.original.Position
		local anchor = mount.anchor and originalPart:FindFirstChild(mount.anchor)
		if anchor and anchor:IsA("Attachment") then position = (AvatarSkins.mountOriginal(state, anchor) or anchor.CFrame).Position end
		local sourceSize, targetSize = originalPart.Size / 2, modelPart.Size / 2
		local scaled = environment.Vector3.new(AvatarSkins.scaleOffset(position.X, sourceSize.X, targetSize.X),
			AvatarSkins.scaleOffset(position.Y, sourceSize.Y, targetSize.Y), AvatarSkins.scaleOffset(position.Z, sourceSize.Z, targetSize.Z))
		saved.applied = source:Inverse() * target * environment.CFrame.new(scaled - position) * saved.original
		if not current:FuzzyEq(saved.applied, 0.0001) then object[mount.property] = saved.applied end
	end
end

function AvatarSkins.collectBody(state, character)
	local body = {}
	for _, child in character:GetChildren() do
		if child:IsA("Tool") or child:GetAttribute("PulseVFX") then continue end
		if child:IsA("BasePart") or child:IsA("Decal") then table.insert(body, child) end
		for _, object in child:GetDescendants() do
			if (object:IsA("BasePart") or object:IsA("Decal")) and not object:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") then table.insert(body, object) end
		end
	end
	state.body, state.bodyAt, state.bodyDirty = body, state.context.environment.os.clock(), false
	if state.bodyCharacter == character then return end
	if state.bodyConnection then pcall(function() state.bodyConnection:Disconnect() end) end
	state.bodyCharacter = character
	state.bodyConnection = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function() state.bodyDirty = true end)
end

function AvatarSkins.hideBody(state, character)
	local age = state.context.environment.os.clock() - (state.bodyAt or 0)
	if state.bodyCharacter ~= character or not state.body or state.bodyDirty and age > 0.2 or age > 3 then AvatarSkins.collectBody(state, character) end
	for _, object in state.body do
		if not object.Parent or object.LocalTransparencyModifier == 1 then continue end
		object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
		state.hidden[object] = true
	end
end

function AvatarSkins.showBody(state)
	for object in state.hidden do pcall(function() if object.Parent then object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end end) end
	table.clear(state.hidden)
	state.body = nil
end

function AvatarSkins.firstPerson(state, character)
	local camera = state.context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local globals = state.context.environment.getgenv()
	local invis = rawget(globals, "__pulseInvis")
	local near = camera and head and (camera.CFrame.Position - head.Position).Magnitude < 1.4
	local transparency = near and 1 or type(invis) == "table" and invis.active == true and 0.5 or 0
	if transparency == state.near then return end
	state.near = transparency
	for _, part in state.modelParts do if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = transparency end end
end

function AvatarSkins.pose(state, map, targetJoints, root)
	if not state.links or not root then return false end
	local rootLink
	for _, link in state.links do
		local relative = link.restTarget
		if link.root then
			rootLink = link
		else
			local first, second = map(link.source.parent), map(link.source.part)
			if first and second and first.Parent and second.Parent then
				relative = link.pivot0 * (first.CFrame:Inverse() * second.CFrame).Rotation * link.pivot1
			end
		end
		local joint = link.target
		if targetJoints then joint = targetJoints[link.target.part.Name] end
		if joint then joint.joint.Transform = link.inverse * relative * link.target1 end
		if not targetJoints then link.relativeTarget = relative end
	end
	local source = rootLink and map(rootLink.source.part)
	if not source or not source.Parent then return false end
	root.CFrame = source.CFrame * rootLink.restTarget:Inverse()
	return true
end

function AvatarSkins.signature(character)
	local torso = character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
	local shirt, pants = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt"), character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
	return table.concat({torso and torso:IsA("MeshPart") and torso.MeshId or "", shirt and shirt.ShirtTemplate or "",
		pants and pants.PantsTemplate or ""}, "|")
end

function AvatarSkins.freeCorpse(corpse)
	if corpse.watch then pcall(function() corpse.watch:Disconnect() end) end
	if corpse.model then pcall(function() corpse.model:Destroy() end) end
	for _, object in corpse.hidden do pcall(function() if object.Parent then object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end end) end
end

function AvatarSkins.adoptCorpse(state, ragdoll)
	if state.corpses[ragdoll] or not state.model or not state.links then return end
	local model = state.model:Clone()
	model.Name = state.remote and "PulseRemoteSkinCorpse" or "PulseSkinCorpse"
	local corpse = {model = model, map = {}, hidden = {}, seen = {}, fade = -1, links = state.links,
		started = state.context.environment.os.clock(), parts = {}}
	for _, part in ragdoll:GetChildren() do if part:IsA("BasePart") then corpse.map[part.Name] = part end end
	corpse.joints = AvatarSkins.joints(model)
	for _, part in model:GetDescendants() do
		if not part:IsA("BasePart") then continue end
		AvatarSkins.ghost(part)
		if part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then table.insert(corpse.parts, part) end
	end
	corpse.root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	model.Parent = state.context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	state.corpses[ragdoll] = corpse
end

function AvatarSkins.updateCorpses(state)
	local now = state.context.environment.os.clock()
	for ragdoll, corpse in state.corpses do
		if not ragdoll.Parent or not corpse.model.Parent then
			AvatarSkins.freeCorpse(corpse)
			state.corpses[ragdoll] = nil
			continue
		end
		if corpse.dirty ~= false or now - (corpse.scanAt or 0) > 1 then
			corpse.dirty, corpse.scanAt = false, now
			if not corpse.watch then corpse.watch = ragdoll.DescendantAdded:Connect(function() corpse.dirty = true end) end
			for _, object in ragdoll:GetDescendants() do
				if not object:IsA("BasePart") and not object:IsA("Decal") then continue end
				object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
				if not corpse.seen[object] then corpse.seen[object] = true; table.insert(corpse.hidden, object) end
			end
		end
		local torso = corpse.map.LowerTorso
		if not corpse.done then
			AvatarSkins.pose({links = corpse.links}, function(part) return corpse.map[part.Name] end, corpse.joints, corpse.root)
			if torso and torso.Anchored and now - corpse.started > 5 then corpse.done = true end
		end
		local fade = 0
		for _, object in corpse.hidden do
			if object.Parent and object:IsA("BasePart") and object.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then fade = math.max(fade, object.Transparency) end
		end
		if fade == corpse.fade then continue end
		corpse.fade = fade
		for _, part in corpse.parts do if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = fade end end
	end
end

function AvatarSkins.watchRagdoll(state, object)
	if object.Name ~= "Raggy" or not object:IsA("Model") then return end
	local now, recent = state.context.environment.os.clock(), {}
	for _, entry in state.ragdolls do if now - entry.time < 6 then table.insert(recent, entry) end end
	table.insert(recent, {model = object, time = now})
	state.ragdolls = recent
end

function AvatarSkins.matchDeath(state)
	local death = state.death
	if not death or death.done then return end
	local now = state.context.environment.os.clock()
	if now - death.time > 4 then death.done = true; return end
	local recent = {}
	local candidates = state.registry and state.registry.ragdolls or state.ragdolls
	for _, entry in candidates do
		local ragdoll = entry.model
		if now - entry.time >= 6 or not ragdoll.Parent then continue end
		table.insert(recent, entry)
		if state.registry and entry.taken then continue end
		local root = ragdoll:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or ragdoll:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local ok, signature = pcall(AvatarSkins.signature, ragdoll)
		if not root or (root.Position - death.position).Magnitude >= 12 then continue end
		if death.signature ~= "||" and (not ok or signature ~= death.signature) then continue end
		death.done, death.corpse = true, ragdoll
		if state.registry then entry.taken = true end
		AvatarSkins.adoptCorpse(state, ragdoll)
		if state.registry then return end
	end
	if not state.registry then state.ragdolls = recent end
end

function AvatarSkins.deadPose(state, character, camera)
	AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state)
	AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(state)
	local root = state.model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local replaced = state.death and state.death.corpse and state.corpses[state.death.corpse]
	local posed = not replaced and AvatarSkins.pose(state, function(part) return part end, nil, root)
	state.model.Parent = posed and camera or nil
	AvatarSkins.hideBody(state, character)
	local transparency = 0
	for _, object in state.body do
		if object.Parent and object:IsA("BasePart") and object.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then transparency = math.max(transparency, object.Transparency) end
	end
	if transparency == state.deadFade then return end
	state.deadFade, state.near = transparency, nil
	for _, part in state.modelParts do if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = transparency end end
end

function AvatarSkins.step(state)
	local model = state.model
	if not model then return end
	local character, camera = state.context.player.Character, state.context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	pcall(AvatarSkins.matchDeath, state)
	pcall(AvatarSkins.updateCorpses, state)
	if character and humanoid and character.Parent and humanoid.Health <= 0 and state.character == character and state.links then
		if not state.death or state.death.character ~= character then
			local root = character:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			local ok, signature = pcall(AvatarSkins.signature, character)
			state.death = {character = character, time = state.context.environment.os.clock(),
				position = root and root.Position or state.context.environment.Vector3.zero, signature = ok and signature or "||"}
			pcall(AvatarSkins.matchDeath, state)
		end
		AvatarSkins.deadPose(state, character, camera)
		return
	end
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not root then
		model.Parent = nil
		AvatarSkins.showBody(state)
		AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state)
		AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(state)
		state.character, state.links = nil, nil
		return
	end
	if state.deadFade then
		state.deadFade, state.near = nil, nil
		if state.remote then for _, part in state.modelParts do if part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0 end end end
	end
	local now = state.context.environment.os.clock()
	if state.character ~= character or not state.links and now - (state.attachAt or 0) > 0.5 then
		state.attachAt = now
		state.driven = false
		AvatarSkins.showBody(state)
		AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state)
		AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(state)
		AvatarSkins.attach(state, character)
	end
	if not state.links then return end
	if model.Parent ~= camera then
		model.Parent = camera
		state.driven = false
		for _, part in model:GetDescendants() do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and (part.CanCollide or part.CanTouch or part.CanQuery) then
				part.CanCollide, part.CanTouch, part.CanQuery = false, false, false
			end
		end
	end
	if state.remote and state.driven and camera then
		local distance = (root.Position - camera.CFrame.Position).Magnitude
		if distance > 12 then
			local point, size = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position), camera.ViewportSize
			if point.Z < -6 or point.X < -size.X * 0.35 or point.X > size.X * 1.35 or point.Y < -size.Y * 0.35 or point.Y > size.Y * 1.35 then return end
			if distance > 100 then
				state.tick = (state.tick or 0) + 1
				if state.tick % (distance > 250 and 3 or 2) ~= 0 then return end
			end
		end
	end
	if not AvatarSkins.drive(state, character) then return end
	state.driven = true
	AvatarSkins.hideBody(state, character)
	pcall(AvatarSkins.grip, state, character)
	pcall(AvatarSkins.followMounts, state, character)
	if not state.remote then AvatarSkins.firstPerson(state, character) end
end

function AvatarSkins.bind(state, enabled)
	if enabled == state.bound then return end
	state.bound = enabled
	local runService = state.context.runService
	if not enabled then
		pcall(runService.UnbindFromRenderStep, runService, "PulseSkinStep")
		if state.ragdollConnection then state.ragdollConnection:Disconnect(); state.ragdollConnection = nil end
		return
	end
	local priority = state.context.environment.Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2
	runService:BindToRenderStep("PulseSkinStep", priority, function()
		if not state.alive then return end
		if not pcall(AvatarSkins.step, state) then state.links = nil end
	end)
	local added = state.context.workspace.DescendantAdded
	if added then state.ragdollConnection = added:Connect(function(object) AvatarSkins.watchRagdoll(state, object) end) end
end

function AvatarSkins.stop(state, suppressNotify)
	state.token += 1
	AvatarSkins.bind(state, false)
	AvatarSkins.showBody(state)
	AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state)
	AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(state)
	for ragdoll, corpse in state.corpses do AvatarSkins.freeCorpse(corpse); state.corpses[ragdoll] = nil end
	state.death, state.ragdolls = nil, {}
	if state.bodyConnection then pcall(function() state.bodyConnection:Disconnect() end); state.bodyConnection = nil end
	state.bodyCharacter = nil
	local model = state.model
	state.model, state.character, state.links, state.joints, state.ground = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
	state.baseDesc, state.public, state.near, state.legOn = nil, nil, nil, nil
	if model then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end); if not suppressNotify then AvatarSkins.notify(state) end end
end

function AvatarSkins.wear(state, description)
	if not state.alive then return false, "cancelled" end
	state.token += 1
	local token = state.token
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local ok, model = pcall(context.players.CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription, context.players, description, environment.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
	if token ~= state.token or not state.alive then
		if ok and model then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end) end
		return false, "cancelled"
	end
	if not ok or not model then return false, "Couldn't build: " .. tostring(model) end
	local prepared, joints, ground, parts = pcall(AvatarSkins.prepare, state, model)
	if not prepared or not ground then
		pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
		return false, "Couldn't rig: " .. tostring(joints)
	end
	local previous = state.model
	AvatarSkins.showBody(state)
	AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(state)
	AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(state)
	state.model, state.joints, state.ground, state.modelParts = model, joints, ground, parts
	state.character, state.links, state.near = nil, nil, nil
	if previous then pcall(function() previous:Destroy() end) end
	AvatarSkins.bind(state, true)
	return true
end

function AvatarSkins.headless(model, enabled)
	local head = model and model:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if not head then return end
	local objects = {head}
	for _, child in head:GetChildren() do if child:IsA("Decal") then table.insert(objects, child) end end
	for _, object in objects do pcall(function()
		local original = object:GetAttribute("PulseHL")
		if enabled then
			if original == nil then object:SetAttribute("PulseHL", object.Transparency) end
			object.Transparency = 1
			return
		end
		if original ~= nil then object.Transparency = original; object:SetAttribute("PulseHL", nil) end
	end) end
end

function AvatarSkins.cosmetics(state)
	local callback = rawget(state.context.environment.getgenv(), "__pulseCosMine")
	if type(callback) ~= "function" then return false, false end
	local ok, leg, head = pcall(callback)
	return ok and leg == true, ok and head == true
end

function AvatarSkins.dress(state, description)
	state.baseDesc = description
	local leg, head = AvatarSkins.cosmetics(state)
	local modelDescription = description
	if leg then
		local ok, cloned = pcall(function() return description:Clone() end)
		if ok and cloned then
			modelDescription = cloned
			pcall(function() cloned.RightLeg = 139607718 end)
		else
			leg = false
		end
	end
	local ok, reason = AvatarSkins.wear(state, modelDescription)
	if ok then state.legOn = leg; AvatarSkins.headless(state.model, head) end
	return ok, reason
end

function AvatarSkins.applyBundle(state, value)
	local id = tonumber(value)
	if not id then return false, "Bad bundle id" end
	if not state.context.player.Character then return false, "No character" end
	local description, name = AvatarSkins.bundle(state.context, id)
	if not description then return false, tostring(name) end
	local ok, reason = AvatarSkins.dress(state, description)
	if not ok then return false, tostring(reason) end
	state.public = {b = id}
	state.config.skin = {kind = "bundle", id = tostring(id), name = name, desc = AvatarSkins.serialize(description)}
	state.save()
	return true, tostring(name)
end

function AvatarSkins.applyUser(state, value)
	local query = tostring(value or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
	if query == "" then return false, "Enter a username" end
	if not state.context.player.Character then return false, "No character" end
	local ok, description, name, id = pcall(AvatarSkins.user, state.context, query)
	if not ok or not description then return false, "Player not found" end
	local dressed, reason = AvatarSkins.dress(state, description)
	if not dressed then return false, tostring(reason) end
	state.public = tonumber(id) and {u = tonumber(id)} or nil
	state.config.skin = {kind = "user", id = query, name = name, uid = tonumber(id), desc = AvatarSkins.serialize(description)}
	state.save()
	return true, tostring(name)
end

function AvatarSkins.applySaved(state, saved)
	if type(saved) ~= "table" then return false end
	local description = AvatarSkins.deserialize(state.context.environment, saved.desc)
	if description then
		local ok = AvatarSkins.dress(state, description)
		if ok then
			local id = tonumber(saved.kind == "bundle" and saved.id or saved.uid or saved.id)
			state.public = id and (saved.kind == "bundle" and {b = id} or {u = id}) or nil
			state.config.skin = saved
			state.save()
			return true
		end
	end
	if saved.kind == "user" then return AvatarSkins.applyUser(state, saved.id) end
	if saved.id then return AvatarSkins.applyBundle(state, saved.id) end
	return false
end

function AvatarSkins.install(context, config, save)
	local state = {context = context, config = config, save = save, token = 0, alive = true, bound = false,
		hidden = {}, grips = {}, mounts = {}, corpses = {}, ragdolls = {}}
	local globals = context.environment.getgenv()
	local model = function() return state.model and state.links and state.model.Parent and state.model or nil end
	local mine = function() return state.model and state.public or nil end
	local mountOriginal = function(object)
		local original = AvatarSkins.mountOriginal(state, object)
		if original then return original end
		local remote = rawget(globals, "__pulseRemoteSkin")
		for _, entry in remote and remote.list or {} do
			original = AvatarSkins.mountOriginal(entry, object)
			if original then return original end
		end
	end
	local cosmetics = function()
		context.environment.task.spawn(function()
			if not state.alive or not state.model or not state.baseDesc then return end
			local leg, head = AvatarSkins.cosmetics(state)
			if leg ~= (state.legOn == true) then AvatarSkins.dress(state, state.baseDesc); return end
			AvatarSkins.headless(state.model, head)
		end)
	end
	globals.__pulseSkinModel, globals.__pulseSkinMine, globals.__pulseMountOrig, globals.__pulseSkinCos = model, mine, mountOriginal, cosmetics
	return state, function()
		state.alive = false
		AvatarSkins.stop(state)
		if globals.__pulseSkinModel == model then globals.__pulseSkinModel = nil end
		if globals.__pulseSkinMine == mine then globals.__pulseSkinMine = nil end
		if globals.__pulseMountOrig == mountOriginal then globals.__pulseMountOrig = nil end
		if globals.__pulseSkinCos == cosmetics then globals.__pulseSkinCos = nil end
	end
end

end -- AvatarSkins private scope

local RemoteAvatarSkins = {}

function RemoteAvatarSkins.bind(state)
	local enabled = next(state.list) ~= nil
	if enabled == state.bound then return end
	state.bound = enabled
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	if not enabled then
		pcall(context.runService.UnbindFromRenderStep, context.runService, "PulseRSkinStep")
		if state.ragdollConnection then state.ragdollConnection:Disconnect(); state.ragdollConnection = nil end
		state.ragdolls = {}
		return
	end
	local priority = environment.Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 3
	context.runService:BindToRenderStep("PulseRSkinStep", priority, function()
		for _, entry in state.list do if not pcall(AvatarSkins.step, entry) then entry.links = nil end end
	end)
	state.ragdollConnection = context.workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(object) AvatarSkins.watchRagdoll(state, object) end)
end

function RemoteAvatarSkins.clear(state, player)
	local entry = state.list[player]
	if not entry then return end
	state.list[player] = nil
	entry.alive = false
	entry.generation += 1
	AvatarSkins.stop(entry, true)
	RemoteAvatarSkins.bind(state)
	AvatarSkins.notify(entry)
end

function RemoteAvatarSkins.description(state, selection)
	local key = selection.u and "u" .. selection.u or "b" .. selection.b
	if state.descriptions[key] then return state.descriptions[key] end
	local description
	if selection.u then
		local ok, value = pcall(state.context.players.GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId, state.context.players, selection.u)
		if ok then description = value end
	else
		local ok, value = pcall(AvatarSkins.bundle, state.context, selection.b)
		if ok then description = value end
	end
	if description then state.descriptions[key] = description end
	return description
end

function RemoteAvatarSkins.build(state, player, entry, selection, leg)
	entry.token += 1
	local token = entry.token
	local description = RemoteAvatarSkins.description(state, selection)
	if token ~= entry.token or state.list[player] ~= entry then return end
	if not description then entry.failAt = state.context.environment.os.clock(); return end
	if leg then
		local ok, cloned = pcall(function() return description:Clone() end)
		if ok and cloned then
			description = cloned
			pcall(function() cloned.RightLeg = 139607718 end)
		end
	end
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local ok, model = pcall(context.players.CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription, context.players, description, environment.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
	if token ~= entry.token or state.list[player] ~= entry then
		if ok and model then pcall(function() model:Destroy() end) end
		return
	end
	if not ok or not model then entry.failAt = environment.os.clock(); return end
	local prepared, joints, ground, parts = pcall(AvatarSkins.prepare, entry, model)
	if not prepared or not ground then
		pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
		entry.failAt = environment.os.clock()
		return
	end
	model.Name = "PulseRemoteSkin"
	AvatarSkins.showBody(entry)
	AvatarSkins.restoreGrips(entry)
	AvatarSkins.restoreMounts(entry)
	if entry.model then pcall(function() entry.model:Destroy() end) end
	entry.model, entry.joints, entry.ground, entry.modelParts = model, joints, ground, parts
	entry.character, entry.links = nil, nil
	AvatarSkins.headless(model, entry.headless)
	entry.headlessApplied = entry.headless
end

function RemoteAvatarSkins.set(state, player, payload, leg, headless)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	if environment.typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or player == context.player then return end
	local user = type(payload) == "table" and tonumber(payload.u) or nil
	local bundle = type(payload) == "table" and tonumber(payload.b) or nil
	if user and (user < 1 or user >= 10000000000000) then user = nil end
	if bundle and (bundle < 1 or bundle >= 10000000000000) then bundle = nil end
	if not user and not bundle then RemoteAvatarSkins.clear(state, player); return end
	local selection = user and {u = math.floor(user)} or {b = math.floor(bundle)}
	local key = (user and "u" .. selection.u or "b" .. selection.b) .. (leg and "|k" or "")
	local entry = state.list[player]
	if entry and entry.key == key then
		entry.headless = headless == true
		if entry.model and entry.headlessApplied ~= entry.headless then
			AvatarSkins.headless(entry.model, entry.headless)
			entry.headlessApplied = entry.headless
		end
		if entry.model or entry.busy or entry.failAt and environment.os.clock() - entry.failAt < 30 then return end
	end
	if entry then
		AvatarSkins.stop(entry, true)
	else
		local entryContext = table.clone(context)
		entryContext.player = player
		entry = {context = entryContext, player = player, remote = true, registry = state, alive = true, bound = false, token = 0,
			generation = 0, hidden = {}, grips = {}, mounts = {}, corpses = {}, ragdolls = {}}
		state.list[player] = entry
	end
	entry.key, entry.headless, entry.failAt = key, headless == true, nil
	entry.generation += 1
	local generation = entry.generation
	entry.busy = true
	RemoteAvatarSkins.bind(state)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		if entry.generation ~= generation or state.list[player] ~= entry then return end
		pcall(RemoteAvatarSkins.build, state, player, entry, selection, leg)
		if entry.generation == generation then entry.busy = false end
	end)
end

function RemoteAvatarSkins.install(context)
	local state = {context = context, list = {}, descriptions = {}, ragdolls = {}, bound = false}
	state.set = function(player, payload, leg, headless) RemoteAvatarSkins.set(state, player, payload, leg, headless) end
	state.clear = function(player) RemoteAvatarSkins.clear(state, player) end
	state.clearAll = function() for player in state.list do RemoteAvatarSkins.clear(state, player) end end
	state.modelOf = function(player) local entry = state.list[player]; return entry and entry.model or nil end
	state.liveModel = function(player)
		local entry = state.list[player]
		if not entry or not entry.model or not entry.links or not entry.model.Parent or entry.character ~= player.Character then return nil end
		return entry.model
	end
	local globals = context.environment.getgenv()
	globals.__pulseRemoteSkin = state
	local removing
	if context.players.PlayerRemoving then removing = context.connect(context.players.PlayerRemoving, state.clear) end
	return state, function()
		if removing then pcall(function() removing:Disconnect() end) end
		state.clearAll()
		if globals.__pulseRemoteSkin == state then globals.__pulseRemoteSkin = nil end
	end
end

local AnimationFeature = {}
do -- Keep module-private locals out of the main chunk's register budget.

local ANIMATION_SLOTS = {
	idle = "IdleAnimation", walk = "WalkAnimation", run = "RunAnimation", jump = "JumpAnimation",
	fall = "FallAnimation", climb = "ClimbAnimation", swim = "SwimAnimation", swimidle = "SwimAnimation",
}
local ANIMATION_TYPES = {IdleAnimation = true, WalkAnimation = true, RunAnimation = true, JumpAnimation = true,
	FallAnimation = true, ClimbAnimation = true, SwimAnimation = true}
local ANIMATION_ORDER = {"IdleAnimation", "WalkAnimation", "RunAnimation", "JumpAnimation", "FallAnimation", "ClimbAnimation", "SwimAnimation"}

function AnimationFeature.defaults(saved)
	saved = type(saved) == "table" and saved or {}
	return {
		favorites = type(saved.favorites) == "table" and saved.favorites or {}, lastBundle = saved.lastBundle,
		speed = tonumber(saved.speed) or 1, emoteSpeed = tonumber(saved.emoteSpeed) or 1,
		mix = type(saved.mix) == "table" and saved.mix or {}, mixSaves = type(saved.mixSaves) == "table" and saved.mixSaves or {},
		skinMix = type(saved.skinMix) == "table" and saved.skinMix or {}, skin = type(saved.skin) == "table" and saved.skin or nil,
		lastMode = saved.lastMode or "bundle", toggleKey = type(saved.toggleKey) == "string" and saved.toggleKey or "RightShift",
		autoLoad = saved.autoLoad ~= false, emoteHold = saved.emoteHold == true, animPreview = saved.animPreview ~= false,
	}
end

function AnimationFeature.load(context, path)
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		if not context.environment.isfile(path) then return nil end
		return context.http:JSONDecode(context.environment.readfile(path))
	end)
	if ok and type(value) == "table" then return value end
	return {}
end

function AnimationFeature.write(state, path, value)
	pcall(function()
		local environment = state.context.environment
		if not environment.isfolder("BundleAnimations") then environment.makefolder("BundleAnimations") end
		environment.writefile(path, state.context.http:JSONEncode(value))
	end)
end

function AnimationFeature.parseId(value)
	if not value then return nil end
	value = tostring(value)
	return tonumber(value:match("bundles/(%d+)") or value:match("(%d%d%d%d+)") or value:match("(%d+)"))
end

function AnimationFeature.assetAnimations(model, wanted)
	local result = {}
	local animate = model:FindFirstChild("Animate")
	if not animate then return result end
	for _, folder in animate:GetChildren() do
		local kind = ANIMATION_SLOTS[folder.Name:lower()]
		if not kind or wanted and not wanted[kind] then continue end
		for _, animation in folder:GetChildren() do
			if not animation:IsA("Animation") or animation.AnimationId == "" then continue end
			result[kind] = result[kind] or {}
			result[kind][animation.Name] = animation.AnimationId
		end
	end
	return result
end

function AnimationFeature.bundle(state, id, progress)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	id = tonumber(id)
	if not id then return nil end
	local cached = state.cache[tostring(id)]
	if type(cached) == "table" then return cached, state.details[id] end

	local ok, details = pcall(context.avatar.GetItemDetails, context.avatar, id, environment.Enum.AvatarItemType.Bundle)
	if not ok or type(details) ~= "table" or type(details.BundledItems) ~= "table" then return nil end
	state.details[id] = details
	local assets, wanted = {}, {}
	for _, item in details.BundledItems do
		local kind = tostring(item.AssetType)
		if not item.Id or not ANIMATION_TYPES[kind] then continue end
		table.insert(assets, {kind = kind, id = item.Id})
		wanted[kind] = true
	end

	local result
	local description = environment.Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
	for _, asset in assets do pcall(function() description[asset.kind] = tonumber(asset.id) end) end
	local created, model = pcall(context.players.CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription, context.players, description, environment.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
	description:Destroy()
	if created and model then
		pcall(function() result = AnimationFeature.assetAnimations(model, wanted) end)
		pcall(function() model:Destroy() end)
	end
	if result and next(result) and progress then pcall(progress, #assets, #assets) end
	if not result or not next(result) then
		result = {}
		for index, asset in assets do
			pcall(function()
				for _, model in environment.game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. asset.id) do
					for _, animation in model:GetDescendants() do
						if not animation:IsA("Animation") then continue end
						result[asset.kind] = result[asset.kind] or {}
						result[asset.kind][animation.Name] = animation.AnimationId
					end
				end
			end)
			if progress then pcall(progress, index, #assets) end
			environment.task.wait()
		end
	end
	if not next(result) then return nil, details end
	state.cache[tostring(id)] = result
	AnimationFeature.write(state, "BundleAnimations/anims_cache.json", state.cache)
	return result, details
end

function AnimationFeature.stop(state)
	state.token += 1
	local emote = state.gameEmote
	state.gameEmote = nil
	if emote then pcall(function() emote:Stop(0) end) end
	for _, connection in state.connections do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
	table.clear(state.connections)
	state.toolSync, state.resume = nil, nil
	for _, track in state.tracks do pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end) end
	table.clear(state.tracks)
	state.current = nil
	local backup = state.backup
	if not backup then return end
	for animation, id in backup.ids do pcall(function() if animation.Parent then animation.AnimationId = id end end) end
	pcall(function() if backup.animate.Parent then backup.animate.Disabled = backup.disabled end end)
	state.backup = nil
end

function AnimationFeature.backup(state, animate)
	if not animate then return end
	local backup = {animate = animate, disabled = animate.Disabled, ids = {}}
	state.backup = backup
	animate.Disabled = true
	for _, folder in animate:GetChildren() do
		if not ANIMATION_SLOTS[folder.Name:lower()] then continue end
		for _, animation in folder:GetChildren() do
			if not animation:IsA("Animation") then continue end
			local id = animation.AnimationId
			if id == "" then id = animation:GetAttribute("PulseOrigAnim") or "" else animation:SetAttribute("PulseOrigAnim", id) end
			backup.ids[animation] = id
			animation.AnimationId = ""
		end
	end
end

function AnimationFeature.track(state, animator, id, priority, looped)
	local digits = tostring(id or ""):match("%d+")
	if not digits or digits == "0" then return nil end
	local animation = state.context.environment.Instance.new("Animation")
	animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. digits
	local ok, track = pcall(animator.LoadAnimation, animator, animation)
	if not ok or not track then return nil end
	track.Looped, track.Priority = looped ~= false, priority
	return track
end

function AnimationFeature.choose(animations, kind, preferred)
	local choices = animations[kind]
	if type(choices) ~= "table" then return nil end
	if preferred and choices[preferred] then return choices[preferred] end
	for _, id in choices do return id end
end

function AnimationFeature.play(state, track, fade, speed)
	if not track then return end
	if state.current ~= track then
		if state.current then pcall(function() state.current:Stop(fade or 0.15) end) end
		state.current = track
		pcall(function() track:Play(fade or 0.15) end)
	end
	if not speed then return end
	state.baseSpeed = speed
	pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(speed * (state.speedMultiplier or state.config.speed)) end)
end

function AnimationFeature.refreshSpeed(state)
	if state.current then pcall(function() state.current:AdjustSpeed(state.baseSpeed * state.speedMultiplier) end) end
end

function AnimationFeature.setSpeed(state, value)
	state.speedMultiplier = tonumber(value) or 1
	AnimationFeature.refreshSpeed(state)
end

function AnimationFeature.stopGameEmote(state, fade)
	local track = state.gameEmote
	state.gameEmote = nil
	if track then pcall(function() track:Stop(fade or 0.2) end) end
end

function AnimationFeature.gameEmoteId(state, animate, animator, input)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local id
	if environment.typeof(input) == "Instance" and input:IsA("Animation") then id = input.AnimationId:match("%d+") end
	if type(input) == "string" and not tonumber(input) then
		local folder = animate and animate:FindFirstChild(input)
		local animation = folder and folder:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation")
		return animation and animation.AnimationId:match("%d+") or nil
	end
	if not id and tonumber(input) then id = tostring(tonumber(input)) end
	if not id then return nil end
	if state.cache["e" .. id] then return state.cache["e" .. id] end

	local preview = AnimationFeature.track(state, animator, id, environment.Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4, false)
	if preview then
		for _ = 1, 8 do
			if preview.Length > 0 then
				state.cache["e" .. id] = id
				pcall(function() preview:Destroy() end)
				return id
			end
			environment.task.wait(0.05)
		end
		pcall(function() preview:Destroy() end)
	end
	return AnimationFeature.emoteId(state, id)
end

function AnimationFeature.playGameEmote(state, animate, animator, token, input)
	if token ~= state.token then return false end
	local id = AnimationFeature.gameEmoteId(state, animate, animator, input)
	if not id or token ~= state.token then return false end
	AnimationFeature.stopGameEmote(state, 0)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local animation = environment.Instance.new("Animation")
	animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
	local ok, track = pcall(animator.LoadAnimation, animator, animation)
	if not ok or not track then return false end
	track.Priority = environment.Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
	state.gameEmote = track
	if state.current then pcall(function() state.current:Stop(0.15) end) end
	state.current, state.pose = nil, "Emote"
	pcall(function() track:Play(0.1) end)
	track.Stopped:Once(function()
		if state.gameEmote ~= track then return end
		state.gameEmote = nil
		if token == state.token and state.resume then state.resume() end
	end)
	return true
end

function AnimationFeature.gameData(state)
	if state.gameSync ~= nil then return state.gameSync or nil end
	local context = state.context
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local database = context.storage:WaitForChild("Database", 5)
		return context.environment.require(database:WaitForChild("Sync", 5))
	end)
	state.gameSync = ok and type(result) == "table" and result or false
	return state.gameSync or nil
end

function AnimationFeature.gameCommand(state, character, humanoid, animate, play, token, command)
	if token ~= state.token or type(command) ~= "string" then return end
	if animate and animate.Parent and not animate.Disabled then return end
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local sync = AnimationFeature.gameData(state)
	local backpack = context.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	local toys = backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Toys")
	local owned = toys and toys:FindFirstChild(command)
	if owned or sync and type(sync.Toys) == "table" and sync.Toys[command] ~= nil then
		local tool
		if owned then
			local remotes = context.storage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
			local extras = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Extras")
			local replicate = extras and extras:FindFirstChild("ReplicateToy")
			if replicate then pcall(function() tool = replicate:InvokeServer(command) end) end
			environment.task.wait()
		else
			tool = backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(command) or character:FindFirstChild(command)
		end
		if environment.typeof(tool) ~= "Instance" or not tool:IsA("Tool") or token ~= state.token then return end
		if tool.Parent ~= character then pcall(function() tool.Parent = backpack end); environment.task.wait() end
		pcall(humanoid.EquipTool, humanoid, tool)
		return
	end
	local emote = sync and type(sync.Emotes) == "table" and sync.Emotes[command]
	if type(emote) ~= "table" then return end
	if type(emote.Command) == "string" and animate and animate:FindFirstChild(emote.Command) then play(emote.Command); return end
	if emote.AnimationID then play(tostring(emote.AnimationID)) end
end

function AnimationFeature.bindGameEmotes(state, character, humanoid, animate, animator, token)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local function play(value) return AnimationFeature.playGameEmote(state, animate, animator, token, value) end
	local bindable = animate and animate:FindFirstChild("PlayEmote")
	if bindable and bindable:IsA("BindableFunction") then
		local previous
		pcall(function() previous = bindable.OnInvoke end)
		local assigned = pcall(function() bindable.OnInvoke = play end)
		if assigned then table.insert(state.connections, {Disconnect = function()
			pcall(function() if bindable.OnInvoke == play then bindable.OnInvoke = previous end end)
		end}) end
	end
	local remotes = context.storage and context.storage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local misc = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Misc")
	local event = misc and misc:FindFirstChild("PlayEmote")
	if event and event:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		table.insert(state.connections, event.Event:Connect(function(command)
			environment.task.spawn(AnimationFeature.gameCommand, state, character, humanoid, animate, play, token, command)
		end))
	end
	if context.player and context.player.Chatted then table.insert(state.connections, context.player.Chatted:Connect(function(message)
		if type(message) ~= "string" or token ~= state.token then return end
		local command = message:match("^/e%s+(%S+)") or message:match("^/emote%s+(%S+)")
		if not command then return end
		local sync = AnimationFeature.gameData(state)
		local emote = sync and type(sync.Emotes) == "table" and sync.Emotes[command]
		if type(emote) == "table" and emote.AnimationID then environment.task.spawn(play, tostring(emote.AnimationID)); return end
		if animate and animate:FindFirstChild(command) then environment.task.spawn(play, command) end
	end)) end
end

function AnimationFeature.bindTools(state, character, humanoid, animate, animator, token)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local priority = environment.Enum.AnimationPriority
	local fallback = humanoid.RigType == environment.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6
		and {toolnone = "182393478", toolslash = "129967390", toollunge = "129967478"}
		or {toolnone = "507768375", toolslash = "522635514", toollunge = "522638767"}
	local function animationId(name)
		local folder = animate and animate:FindFirstChild(name)
		local animation = folder and folder:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animation")
		return animation and animation.AnimationId:match("%d+") or fallback[name]
	end
	local hold = AnimationFeature.track(state, animator, animationId("toolnone"), priority.Action, true)
	state.tracks.toolnone = hold
	local own = {}
	for _, track in state.tracks do own[track] = true end
	local tool, action, toolConnections = nil, nil, {}
	local function updateWeight()
		if not hold or token ~= state.token then return end
		local other = false
		for _, track in animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do
			if own[track] or track == state.gameEmote or not track.IsPlaying or track.WeightTarget <= 0.05 then continue end
			if track.Priority ~= priority.Core and track.Priority.Value >= priority.Action.Value then other = true; break end
		end
		pcall(function() hold:AdjustWeight(other and 0.001 or 1, 0.12) end)
	end
	local function actionRequested(target)
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		local request = target:FindFirstChild("toolanim")
		if not request or not request:IsA("StringValue") then return end
		local value = request.Value
		pcall(function() request:Destroy() end)
		local name = value == "Slash" and "toolslash" or value == "Lunge" and "toollunge"
		if not name then return end
		if action then own[action] = nil; pcall(function() action:Stop(0) end) end
		action = AnimationFeature.track(state, animator, animationId(name), priority.Action2, false)
		if not action then return end
		own[action], state.tracks.toolAct = true, action
		pcall(function() action:Play(0.05) end)
	end
	local function disconnectTool()
		for _, connection in toolConnections do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
		table.clear(toolConnections)
	end
	local function synchronize()
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		local current = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
		if current ~= tool then
			disconnectTool()
			tool = current
			if tool then
				actionRequested(tool)
				table.insert(toolConnections, tool.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
					if child.Name == "toolanim" then environment.task.defer(actionRequested, tool) end
				end))
			end
		end
		if not hold then return end
		if tool and not hold.IsPlaying then pcall(function() hold:Play(0.1) end); updateWeight(); return end
		if not tool and hold.IsPlaying then pcall(function() hold:Stop(0.1) end) end
	end
	state.toolSync = synchronize
	table.insert(state.connections, {Disconnect = disconnectTool})
	for _, signal in {character.ChildAdded, character.ChildRemoved} do
		if not signal then continue end
		table.insert(state.connections, signal:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("Tool") then environment.task.defer(synchronize) end
		end))
	end
	if animator.AnimationPlayed then table.insert(state.connections, animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
		if token ~= state.token or own[track] or not tool then return end
		if track.Priority == priority.Core or track.Priority.Value < priority.Action.Value then return end
		updateWeight()
		track.Stopped:Once(function()
			environment.task.delay(0.05, updateWeight)
			environment.task.delay(0.3, updateWeight)
		end)
	end)) end
	synchronize()
end

function AnimationFeature.applyTracks(state, character, animations)
	AnimationFeature.stop(state)
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
	if not animator then return false end
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local priority = environment.Enum.AnimationPriority
	local definitions = {
		{"idle", "IdleAnimation", "Animation1", priority.Idle}, {"walk", "WalkAnimation", nil, priority.Movement},
		{"run", "RunAnimation", nil, priority.Movement}, {"jump", "JumpAnimation", nil, priority.Movement, false},
		{"fall", "FallAnimation", nil, priority.Movement}, {"climb", "ClimbAnimation", nil, priority.Movement},
		{"swim", "SwimAnimation", "Swim", priority.Movement}, {"swimidle", "SwimAnimation", "SwimIdle", priority.Idle},
	}
	for _, definition in definitions do
		local id = AnimationFeature.choose(animations, definition[2], definition[3])
		state.tracks[definition[1]] = AnimationFeature.track(state, animator, id, definition[4], definition[5])
	end
	local tracks = state.tracks
	if not tracks.idle and not tracks.walk and not tracks.run then return false end
	local animate = character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
	if animate then pcall(AnimationFeature.backup, state, animate) end
	for _, track in animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end) end
	state.pose = "Standing"
	local token = state.token
	local function movementSpeed()
		local root = humanoid.RootPart
		if not root then return 0 end
		local velocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		return math.sqrt(velocity.X * velocity.X + velocity.Z * velocity.Z)
	end
	local function running(speed)
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		if speed > 0.5 then
			AnimationFeature.stopGameEmote(state, 0.1)
			state.pose = "Running"
			AnimationFeature.play(state, speed > 9 and tracks.run or tracks.walk or tracks.run, 0.15, speed / 16)
			return
		end
		if state.gameEmote or state.pose == "Jumping" or state.pose == "FreeFall" then return end
		state.pose = "Standing"
		AnimationFeature.play(state, tracks.idle, 0.2, 1)
	end
	local function connect(signal, callback)
		if signal then table.insert(state.connections, signal:Connect(callback)) end
	end
	state.resume = function() running(movementSpeed()) end
	AnimationFeature.bindGameEmotes(state, character, humanoid, animate, animator, token)
	AnimationFeature.bindTools(state, character, humanoid, animate, animator, token)
	connect(humanoid.Running, running)
	connect(humanoid.Jumping, function()
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		AnimationFeature.stopGameEmote(state, 0.1)
		state.pose = "Jumping"
		AnimationFeature.play(state, tracks.jump or tracks.fall, 0.1, 1)
	end)
	connect(humanoid.FreeFalling, function(active)
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		if active then
			AnimationFeature.stopGameEmote(state, 0.1)
			state.pose = "FreeFall"
			AnimationFeature.play(state, tracks.fall or tracks.jump, 0.25, 1)
			return
		end
		state.pose = "Landed"
		running(movementSpeed())
	end)
	connect(humanoid.Climbing, function(speed)
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		AnimationFeature.stopGameEmote(state, 0.1)
		state.pose = "Climbing"
		AnimationFeature.play(state, tracks.climb, 0.15, speed / 5)
	end)
	connect(humanoid.Swimming, function(speed)
		if token ~= state.token then return end
		state.pose = "Swimming"
		AnimationFeature.play(state, math.abs(speed or 0) > 1 and tracks.swim or tracks.swimidle or tracks.swim, 0.2, 1)
	end)
	connect(humanoid.Seated, function(active) if not active then running(0) end end)
	connect(humanoid.StateChanged, function(_, nextState)
		if token ~= state.token or state.pose ~= "Jumping" and state.pose ~= "FreeFall" then return end
		local states = environment.Enum.HumanoidStateType
		if nextState ~= states.Landed and nextState ~= states.Running and nextState ~= states.RunningNoPhysics then return end
		state.pose = "Landed"
		running(movementSpeed())
	end)
	AnimationFeature.play(state, tracks.idle, 0.2, 1)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		while state.alive and token == state.token do
			environment.task.wait(0.4)
			if token ~= state.token or not state.alive then return end
			if animate and animate.Parent then animate.Disabled = true end
			if state.current and state.current.Looped and not state.current.IsPlaying then pcall(function() state.current:Play(0.1) end) end
			if state.toolSync then pcall(state.toolSync) end
		end
	end)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		while state.alive and token == state.token do
			environment.task.wait(0.1)
			if token ~= state.token or not state.alive then return end
			if not state.gameEmote and state.pose ~= "Jumping" and state.pose ~= "FreeFall" then pcall(running, movementSpeed()) end
		end
	end)
	return true
end

function AnimationFeature.save(state)
	AnimationFeature.write(state, "BundleAnimations/config.json", state.config)
end

function AnimationFeature.enableAutoLoad(state)
	if state.config.autoLoad then return end
	state.config.autoLoad = true
	local setter = state.context.setters and state.context.setters["Free anims/bundles/Auto-load on respawn"]
	if setter then pcall(setter, true) end
end

function AnimationFeature.resetTracks(state)
	AnimationFeature.stop(state)
	local character = state.context.player.Character
	local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
	if not animate then return end
	pcall(function()
		animate.Disabled = true
		state.context.environment.task.wait(0.1)
		animate.Disabled = false
	end)
end

function AnimationFeature.applyBundle(state, value, progress)
	local id = AnimationFeature.parseId(value)
	local character = state.context.player.Character
	if not character or not character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return false, "No character" end
	local animations, details = AnimationFeature.bundle(state, id, progress)
	if not animations then return false, "Failed to load bundle animations (ID: " .. tostring(id) .. ")" end
	if not AnimationFeature.applyTracks(state, character, animations) then return false, "Couldn't apply (no Animator)" end
	state.config.lastBundle, state.config.lastMode = tostring(id), "bundle"
	AnimationFeature.enableAutoLoad(state)
	AnimationFeature.save(state)
	return true, "Applied (visible to all): " .. (details and details.Name or "ID " .. id)
end

function AnimationFeature.applyMix(state)
	local character = state.context.player.Character
	if not character or not character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then return false, "No character" end
	local assigned = false
	for _, kind in ANIMATION_ORDER do if state.config.mix[kind] then assigned = true; break end end
	if not assigned then return false, "Assign at least one slot first (tap SET)" end
	local animations = AnimationFeature.assetAnimations(character)
	local cache = {}
	for _, kind in ANIMATION_ORDER do
		local slot = state.config.mix[kind]
		local id = slot and tonumber(slot.id)
		if not id then continue end
		if cache[id] == nil then cache[id] = AnimationFeature.bundle(state, id) or false end
		if cache[id] and cache[id][kind] then animations[kind] = cache[id][kind] end
	end
	if not AnimationFeature.applyTracks(state, character, animations) then return false, "Couldn't apply mix (no Animator)" end
	state.config.lastMode = "mix"
	AnimationFeature.enableAutoLoad(state)
	AnimationFeature.save(state)
	return true, "Applied mix (visible to all)"
end

function AnimationFeature.setMixSlot(state, kind, id, name)
	state.config.mix[kind] = {id = tostring(id), name = name}
	AnimationFeature.save(state)
end

function AnimationFeature.clearMixSlot(state, kind)
	state.config.mix[kind] = nil
	AnimationFeature.save(state)
end

function AnimationFeature.clearMix(state)
	state.config.mix = {}
	AnimationFeature.save(state)
	if state.config.lastMode == "mix" then state.reset() end
end

function AnimationFeature.copyMix(mix)
	local copy = {}
	for key, value in type(mix) == "table" and mix or {} do
		if type(value) == "table" then copy[key] = {id = value.id, name = value.name} end
	end
	return copy
end

function AnimationFeature.queueMix(state)
	state.mixGeneration = (state.mixGeneration or 0) + 1
	local generation = state.mixGeneration
	state.context.environment.task.delay(0.05, function()
		if not state.alive or state.mixGeneration ~= generation then return end
		if next(state.config.mix) then pcall(AnimationFeature.applyMix, state); return end
		if state.config.lastMode == "mix" then state.reset() end
	end)
end

function AnimationFeature.stopEmote(state)
	for _, connection in state.emoteConnections do pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
	table.clear(state.emoteConnections)
	if state.emote then pcall(function() state.emote:Stop(0.2) end) end
	state.emote = nil
end

function AnimationFeature.emoteId(state, value)
	local id = tostring(value or ""):match("%d+")
	if not id then return nil end
	local key = "e" .. id
	if state.cache[key] then return state.cache[key] end

	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local character = context.player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local animate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Animate")
	local resolved
	if humanoid and animate then
		local previous = animate:FindFirstChild("PlayEmote")
		local proxy = environment.Instance.new("BindableFunction")
		proxy.OnInvoke = function(animation)
			if environment.typeof(animation) == "Instance" and animation:IsA("Animation") then resolved = animation.AnimationId:match("%d+") end
			return false
		end
		if previous then previous.Name = "PlayEmote_PulseHold" end
		proxy.Name, proxy.Parent = "PlayEmote", animate
		pcall(humanoid.PlayEmoteAndGetAnimTrackById, humanoid, tonumber(id))
		proxy:Destroy()
		if previous and previous.Parent then previous.Name = "PlayEmote" end
	end
	if not resolved then
		pcall(function()
			for _, model in environment.game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://" .. id) do
				local objects = model:GetDescendants()
				table.insert(objects, model)
				for _, animation in objects do
					if not animation:IsA("Animation") then continue end
					resolved = animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")
					if resolved then return end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	if resolved then
		state.cache[key] = resolved
		AnimationFeature.write(state, "BundleAnimations/anims_cache.json", state.cache)
	end
	return resolved or id
end

function AnimationFeature.applyEmote(state, value, name)
	local character = state.context.player.Character
	local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
	if not animator then return false, "No character" end
	local id = AnimationFeature.emoteId(state, value)
	if not id then return false, "Couldn't load emote" end
	AnimationFeature.stopEmote(state)
	local track = AnimationFeature.track(state, animator, id, state.context.environment.Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4, true)
	if not track then return false, "Couldn't load emote" end
	state.emote = track
	pcall(function() track:Play(0.15); track:AdjustSpeed(state.config.emoteSpeed) end)
	for _, signal in {humanoid.Running, humanoid.Jumping} do
		if not signal then continue end
		table.insert(state.emoteConnections, signal:Connect(function(value)
			if state.config.emoteHold then return end
			if value == true or type(value) == "number" and value > 0.5 then AnimationFeature.stopEmote(state) end
		end))
	end
	return true, "Playing emote: " .. (name or "ID " .. id)
end

function AnimationFeature.applyConfig(state, saved)
	local restored = AnimationFeature.defaults(saved)
	for key in state.config do state.config[key] = nil end
	for key, value in restored do state.config[key] = value end
	AnimationFeature.save(state)
	AnimationFeature.setSpeed(state, state.config.speed)
	AnimationFeature.stopEmote(state)
	state.generation += 1
	local generation = state.generation
	local lastMode, lastBundle = state.config.lastMode, state.config.lastBundle
