	local skin = state.config.skin
	state.context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		if not state.alive or state.generation ~= generation then return end
		if state.skin then
			AvatarSkins.stop(state.skin)
			if type(skin) == "table" then pcall(AvatarSkins.applySaved, state.skin, skin) end
		end
		if not state.alive or state.generation ~= generation then return end
		if lastMode == "mix" and next(state.config.mix) then
			pcall(AnimationFeature.applyMix, state)
		else
			if lastBundle then pcall(AnimationFeature.applyBundle, state, lastBundle)
			else AnimationFeature.resetTracks(state) end
		end
		if not state.alive or state.generation ~= generation then return end
		state.config.lastMode, state.config.lastBundle, state.config.skin = lastMode, lastBundle, skin
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
		if state.uiRefresh then pcall(state.uiRefresh) end
		if state.favRefresh then pcall(state.favRefresh) end
	end)
end

function AnimationFeature.controls(state)
	local section, context, config = state.context.section, state.context, state.config
	section:Sub("bundles")
	section:Header("Animation options")
	local speed = section:Slider({Text = "Animation Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 3, Default = config.speed, Decimals = 2, Callback = function(value)
		config.speed = value
		AnimationFeature.setSpeed(state, value)
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
	end})
	local autoLoad = section:Toggle({Text = "Auto-load on respawn", Default = config.autoLoad, Callback = function(value)
		config.autoLoad = not not value
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Reset to default animations", Callback = state.reset})
	AnimationFeature.catalog(state, "bundle")

	section:Header("Animation combo")
	local slots = {}
	for _, kind in ANIMATION_ORDER do table.insert(slots, {code = kind, label = kind:gsub("Animation$", "")}) end
	local selected, bundleId = ANIMATION_ORDER[1], ""
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Slot", Options = slots, Default = selected, NoSave = true, Callback = function(value) selected = value end})
	local native = section:LastSection()
	if native and native.Input then native:Input({Text = "Bundle ID", Live = true, Callback = function(value) bundleId = tostring(value or "") end}) end
	section:Button({Text = "Set slot", Callback = function()
		local id = AnimationFeature.parseId(bundleId)
		if not id then return end
		AnimationFeature.setMixSlot(state, selected, id, "ID " .. id)
		AnimationFeature.queueMix(state)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Clear slot", Callback = function()
		AnimationFeature.clearMixSlot(state, selected)
		AnimationFeature.queueMix(state)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Clear combo", Callback = function() AnimationFeature.clearMix(state) end})

	section:Sub("emotes")
	section:Header("Emote options")
	local emoteSpeed = section:Slider({Text = "Emote Speed", Min = 0.1, Max = 3, Default = config.emoteSpeed, Decimals = 2, Callback = function(value)
		config.emoteSpeed = value
		if state.emote then pcall(function() state.emote:AdjustSpeed(value) end) end
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
	end})
	local hold = section:Toggle({Text = "Hold (don't stop when moving)", Default = config.emoteHold, Callback = function(value)
		config.emoteHold = not not value
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Stop emote", Callback = state.stopEmote})
	AnimationFeature.catalog(state, "emote")

	section:Sub("skins")
	section:Header("Skin")
	local username = ""
	native = section:LastSection()
	if native and native.Input then native:Input({Text = "Username or user ID", Live = true,
		Callback = function(value) username = tostring(value or "") end}) end
	section:Button({Text = "Wear", Callback = function()
		context.environment.task.spawn(function()
			local ok, message = AvatarSkins.applyUser(state.skin, username)
			context.window:Notify({Title = context.translation.tr("Skin"), Text = message, Kind = ok and "success" or "danger"})
		end)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Remove skin", Callback = state.resetSkin})
	AnimationFeature.presetControls(state)
	AnimationFeature.catalog(state, "skin")
	AnimationFeature.favoriteControls(state)
	state.uiRefresh = function()
		speed.SetSilent(config.speed)
		autoLoad.SetSilent(config.autoLoad)
		emoteSpeed.SetSilent(config.emoteSpeed)
		hold.SetSilent(config.emoteHold)
	end
end

function AnimationFeature.presetControls(state)
	local context, section = state.context, state.context.section
	local presets = {{code = "163620952", label = "Iysglimt"}, {code = "8990346215", label = "runeaid"},
		{code = "89003145", label = "7788s"}, {code = "1219722", label = "coldness"},
		{code = "1195061435", label = "reaizsym"}, {code = "887330563", label = "aurqzv"}}
	section:Header("Presets")
	local selected = presets[1].code
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Preset", Options = presets, Default = selected, NoSave = true, Callback = function(value) selected = value end})
	section:Button({Text = "Wear preset", Callback = function()
		local choice = selected
		local label
		for _, preset in presets do if preset.code == choice then label = preset.label:lower(); break end end
		local skin = state.config.skin
		local current = type(skin) == "table" and skin.kind == "user" and tostring(skin.id):lower() or nil
		if current and (current == choice or current == label) then state.resetSkin(); return end
		context.environment.task.spawn(function()
			local ok, message = AvatarSkins.applyUser(state.skin, choice)
			context.window:Notify({Title = context.translation.tr("Skin"), Text = message, Kind = ok and "success" or "danger"})
		end)
	end})
end

function AnimationFeature.favoriteControls(state)
	local context, section = state.context, state.context.section
	section:Sub("favorites")
	section:Header("Favorites")
	local selected
	local dropdown = section:Dropdown({Text = "Favorites", Options = {}, Default = "", NoSave = true,
		Callback = function(value) selected = value end})
	state.favRefresh = function()
		local options = {}
		for id, entry in state.config.favorites do
			local kind = type(entry) == "table" and entry.kind or "bundle"
			local name = type(entry) == "table" and entry.name or kind .. " " .. tostring(id)
			table.insert(options, {code = tostring(id), label = tostring(name) .. " (" .. tostring(kind) .. ")"})
		end
		table.sort(options, function(first, second) return first.label < second.label end)
		dropdown.SetOptions(options)
		selected = options[1] and options[1].code or nil
		if selected then dropdown.SetSilent(selected) end
	end
	section:Button({Text = "Apply", Callback = function()
		local entry = selected and state.config.favorites[selected]
		if not entry then return end
		local id = selected
		local kind = type(entry) == "table" and entry.kind or "bundle"
		local name = type(entry) == "table" and entry.name or nil
		context.environment.task.spawn(function()
			if kind == "emote" then AnimationFeature.applyEmote(state, id, name); return end
			if kind == "skin" then AvatarSkins.applyBundle(state.skin, id); return end
			AnimationFeature.applyBundle(state, id)
		end)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Remove favorite", Callback = function()
		if not selected then return end
		state.config.favorites[selected] = nil
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
		state.favRefresh()
	end})
	state.favRefresh()
end

function AnimationFeature.search(state, kind, query)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local params = environment.CatalogSearchParams.new()
	if query ~= "" then params.SearchKeyword = query end
	if kind == "emote" then params.AssetTypes = {environment.Enum.AvatarAssetType.EmoteAnimation}
	else params.BundleTypes = {kind == "skin" and environment.Enum.BundleType.BodyParts or environment.Enum.BundleType.Animations} end
	pcall(function() params.SortType = environment.Enum.CatalogSortType.MostFavorited end)
	local pages = state.context.avatar:SearchCatalog(params)
	local result = {}
	for _ = 1, 4 do
		for _, item in pages:GetCurrentPage() do table.insert(result, {code = tostring(item.Id), label = item.Name or tostring(item.Id)}) end
		if pages.IsFinished then break end
		pages:AdvanceToNextPageAsync()
	end
	return result
end

function AnimationFeature.catalog(state, kind)
	local context, section = state.context, state.context.section
	local text, selected, serial, names = "", nil, 0, {}
	section:Header(kind == "emote" and "Emote catalog" or kind == "skin" and "Skin catalog" or "Animation packs")
	local native = section:LastSection()
	if native and native.Input then native:Input({Text = "Search", Live = true, Callback = function(value) text = tostring(value or "") end}) end
	local dropdown = section:Dropdown({Text = kind == "emote" and "Emotes" or kind == "skin" and "Skins" or "Bundles", Options = {}, Default = "", NoSave = true,
		Callback = function(value) selected = value end})
	local function apply(value)
		if kind == "emote" then return AnimationFeature.applyEmote(state, value, names[tostring(value)]) end
		if kind == "skin" then return AvatarSkins.applyBundle(state.skin, AnimationFeature.parseId(value)) end
		return AnimationFeature.applyBundle(state, value)
	end
	local function notify(ok, message)
		context.window:Notify({Title = context.translation.tr(kind == "emote" and "Emote" or kind == "skin" and "Skin" or "Animation"), Text = message, Kind = ok and "success" or "danger"})
	end
	local function search()
		serial += 1
		local current = serial
		local query = text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
		context.environment.task.spawn(function()
			local ok, results = pcall(AnimationFeature.search, state, kind, query)
			if not state.alive or current ~= serial then return end
			if not ok then notify(false, "Catalog error (rate limit?). Try again."); return end
			names = {}
			for _, result in results do names[result.code] = result.label end
			dropdown.SetOptions(results)
			selected = results[1] and results[1].code or nil
			if selected then dropdown.SetSilent(selected) end
		end)
	end
	section:Button({Text = "Search", Callback = function()
		local value = text:gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
		if value:match("^%d+$") or value:find("bundles/", 1, true) or value:find("catalog/", 1, true) then
			context.environment.task.spawn(function() local ok, message = apply(value); notify(ok, message) end)
			return
		end
		search()
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Apply", Callback = function()
		if not selected then return end
		local id = selected
		context.environment.task.spawn(function() local ok, message = apply(id); notify(ok, message) end)
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Copy ID", Callback = function()
		if not selected then return end
		local copy = context.environment.setclipboard or context.environment.toclipboard
		if copy then pcall(copy, tostring(selected)) end
	end})
	section:Button({Text = "Favorite", Callback = function()
		if not selected then return end
		local favorites = state.config.favorites
		if favorites[selected] then favorites[selected] = nil else favorites[selected] = {name = names[selected] or kind .. " " .. selected, kind = kind} end
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
		if state.favRefresh then state.favRefresh() end
	end})
	search()
end

function AnimationFeature.install(context)
	local environment, globals = context.environment, context.environment.getgenv()
	if globals.__pulseFA_cleanup then pcall(globals.__pulseFA_cleanup) end
	local state = {context = context, alive = true, token = 0, generation = 0, connections = {}, tracks = {}, emoteConnections = {}, details = {},
		config = AnimationFeature.defaults(AnimationFeature.load(context, "BundleAnimations/config.json")),
		cache = AnimationFeature.load(context, "BundleAnimations/anims_cache.json"), baseSpeed = 1}
	state.speedMultiplier = state.config.speed
	state.save = function() AnimationFeature.save(state) end
	local skin, stopSkin = AvatarSkins.install(context, state.config, state.save)
	state.skin = skin
	local remoteSkins, stopRemoteSkins = RemoteAvatarSkins.install(context)
	state.remoteSkins = remoteSkins
	state.applyBundle = function(id, progress) return AnimationFeature.applyBundle(state, id, progress) end
	state.applySkin = function(id) return AvatarSkins.applyBundle(skin, id) end
	state.applySkinUser = function(value) return AvatarSkins.applyUser(skin, value) end
	state.resetSkin = function()
		AvatarSkins.stop(skin)
		state.config.skin = nil
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
		return true, "Skin removed"
	end
	state.applyMix = function() return AnimationFeature.applyMix(state) end
	state.setMixSlot = function(kind, id, name) AnimationFeature.setMixSlot(state, kind, id, name) end
	state.clearMixSlot = function(kind) AnimationFeature.clearMixSlot(state, kind) end
	state.clearMix = function() AnimationFeature.clearMix(state) end
	state.copyMix = AnimationFeature.copyMix
	state.MIX_SLOTS = {}
	for _, kind in ANIMATION_ORDER do table.insert(state.MIX_SLOTS, {key = kind, label = kind:gsub("Animation$", "")}) end
	state.setSpeed = function(value) AnimationFeature.setSpeed(state, value) end
	state.refreshSpeed = function() AnimationFeature.refreshSpeed(state) end
	state.refreshEmoteSpeed = function() if state.emote then pcall(function() state.emote:AdjustSpeed(state.config.emoteSpeed) end) end end
	state.applyEmote = function(id, name) return AnimationFeature.applyEmote(state, id, name) end
	state.stopEmote = function() AnimationFeature.stopEmote(state) end
	state.applyConfig = function(saved) AnimationFeature.applyConfig(state, saved) end
	state.parseBundleId = AnimationFeature.parseId
	state.reset = function()
		AnimationFeature.resetTracks(state)
		state.config.lastBundle = nil
		AnimationFeature.save(state)
		return true, "Default animations restored"
	end
	local function restore()
		if not state.alive or not state.config.autoLoad then return end
		if state.config.lastMode == "mix" and next(state.config.mix) then AnimationFeature.applyMix(state); return end
		if state.config.lastBundle then AnimationFeature.applyBundle(state, state.config.lastBundle) end
	end
	local respawn = context.connect(context.player.CharacterAdded, function(character)
		character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
		environment.task.wait(1)
		if state.alive and context.player.Character == character then pcall(restore) end
	end)
	environment.task.spawn(function() environment.task.wait(0.5); pcall(restore) end)
	if state.config.skin then environment.task.spawn(function()
		local saved = state.config.skin
		for _ = 1, 60 do
			if not state.alive or state.config.skin ~= saved then return end
			local character = context.player.Character
			if character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then break end
			environment.task.wait(0.25)
		end
		if state.alive and state.config.skin == saved then pcall(AvatarSkins.applySaved, skin, saved) end
	end) end
	local stop = function()
		state.alive = false
		state.generation += 1
		pcall(function() respawn:Disconnect() end)
		stopRemoteSkins()
		stopSkin()
		AnimationFeature.stopEmote(state)
		AnimationFeature.stop(state)
		if globals.__pulseFA == state then globals.__pulseFA = nil end
	end
	globals.__pulseFA, globals.__pulseFA_cleanup = state, stop
	return state, stop
end

end -- AnimationFeature private scope

local PulseApplication = {}

PulseApplication.Mobile = {}

function PulseApplication.Mobile.action(state, key)
	local context, settings = state.context, state.context.configuration
	local globals = context.environment.getgenv()
	if key == "fly" then settings.fly.on = not settings.fly.on; return end
	if key == "bhop" then settings.bhop = not settings.bhop; return end
	local toggles = {killall = "KillAll", invis = "Invis", noclip = "Noclip", speed = "Speed", sglitch = "SGlitch"}
	local name = toggles[key]
	if name then
		local get, set = globals["__pulseGet" .. name], globals["__pulseSet" .. name]
		if type(set) == "function" then pcall(set, not (type(get) == "function" and get())) end
		return
	end
	local actions = {shot = "__pulseShotMurder", knife = "__pulseThrowKnife", bombjump = "__pulseAutoBombJump"}
	if actions[key] then
		local action = globals[actions[key]]
		if type(action) == "function" then pcall(action) end
		return
	end
	if key ~= "flingM" and key ~= "flingS" then return end
	if state.flinging then
		state.cancelFling = true
		if type(globals.__pulseFlingCancel) == "function" then pcall(globals.__pulseFlingCancel) end
		return
	end
	state.flinging, state.cancelFling = key, false
	context.environment.task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			local roles = context.roles()
			for _, player in context.players:GetPlayers() do
				if state.cancelFling then break end
				if player == context.player then continue end
				local data = roles[player.Name]
				local role = data and data.Role
				local backpack = player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
				local character = player.Character
				if role == "Innocent" and (character and character:FindFirstChild("Gun") or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")) then role = "Hero" end
				if not data or data.Dead == true or data.Killed == true then continue end
				if key == "flingM" and role ~= "Murderer" then continue end
				if key == "flingS" and role ~= "Sheriff" and role ~= "Hero" then continue end
				local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then continue end
				if type(globals.__pulseFlingPersist) == "function" then
					globals.__pulseFlingPersist(player, {time = 10})
					continue
				end
				if type(globals.__pulseFlingPlayer) == "function" then
					pcall(globals.__pulseFlingPlayer, player)
					while globals.__pulseManualFling and not state.cancelFling do context.environment.task.wait(0.1) end
					continue
				end
				if type(globals.__pulseFlingChar) == "function" then pcall(globals.__pulseFlingChar, character, 1); context.environment.task.wait(0.12) end
			end
		end)
		state.flinging = nil
	end)
end

PulseApplication.Mobile.buttons = {
	{key = "fly", text = "Fly Button", label = "FLY", x = -1, y = 0},
	{key = "bhop", text = "Bhop Button", label = "BHOP", x = 0, y = 0},
	{key = "flingS", text = "Fling Sheriff Button", label = "FLING S", x = 0, y = 1},
	{key = "flingM", text = "Fling Murderer Button", label = "FLING M", x = 1, y = 0},
	{key = "shot", text = "Shot Murder Button", label = "SHOT", x = 0, y = -1},
	{key = "killall", text = "Kill All Murder Button", label = "KILL ALL", x = 2, y = -1},
	{key = "invis", text = "Invisible Button", label = "INVIS", x = -1, y = 1},
	{key = "knife", text = "Knife Throw Button", label = "KNIFE", x = 1, y = -1},
	{key = "bombjump", text = "Auto Bomb Jump Button", label = "BOMB", x = -1, y = 2},
	{key = "noclip", text = "No Clip Button", label = "NOCLIP", x = 1, y = 1},
	{key = "speed", text = "Speed Button", label = "SPEED", x = 2, y = 0},
	{key = "sglitch", text = "Speed Glitch Button", label = "GLITCH", x = 2, y = 1},
}

function PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state)
	if state.painted then table.clear(state.painted) end
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local mobile, style = context.configuration.mobile, context.configuration.uis
	local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
	local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or environment.Vector2.new(800, 600)
	local small = math.min(viewport.X, viewport.Y) <= 500
	local jumpX = viewport.X - (small and 60 or 110)
	local jumpY = viewport.Y - (small and 55 or 150)
	local radius = small and 35 or 60
	for index, item in PulseApplication.Mobile.buttons do
		local button = state.buttons[item.key]
		local size = math.floor(64 * math.clamp((tonumber(style.size[item.key]) or 100) / 100, 0.5, 2) + 0.5)
		local position = mobile.pos[item.key]
		local x = item.x >= 0 and jumpX - radius - 46 - item.x * 76 or jumpX
		local y = item.y >= 0 and jumpY - radius - 46 - item.y * 76 or jumpY
		x, y = math.floor(x - size / 2), math.floor(y - size / 2)
		if style.style == "Compact" then x, y = viewport.X - 20 - size, jumpY - radius - 14 - size - (index - 1) * (size + 8) end
		if type(position) == "table" and type(position.x) == "number" and type(position.y) == "number" then x, y = position.x, position.y end
		button.Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(math.clamp(x, 0, math.max(0, viewport.X - size)), math.clamp(y, 0, math.max(0, viewport.Y - size)))
		button.Size = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(size, size)
		button.Visible = mobile[item.key] == true
		button.BackgroundColor3 = style.style == "Glass" and environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 205, 220) or environment.Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32)
		button.BackgroundTransparency = style.style == "Glass" and 0.65 or 0.15
		button.TextColor3 = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		button.Text = item.label .. (style.lock[item.key] and " рџ”’" or "")
	end
end

function PulseApplication.Mobile.button(state, item)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local inputService = context.inputService
	local button = context.create("TextButton", {Name = item.key, Text = item.label, AutoButtonColor = false,
		Font = environment.Enum.Font.GothamBold, TextSize = 10, BorderSizePixel = 0, Active = true}, state.gui)
	context.corner(button, 32)
	local current, origin, start, moved, doubleTap, lastTime, lastPosition
	context.connect(button.InputBegan, function(input)
		local kind = input.UserInputType
		if kind ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and kind ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
		if current then return end
		current, origin, start, moved = input, input.Position, button.Position, false
		local point = environment.Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
		doubleTap = context.configuration.uis.dtap == true and lastTime ~= nil and environment.os.clock() - lastTime < 0.33
			and (point - lastPosition).Magnitude < 48
		if item.key == "shot" and not doubleTap then PulseApplication.Mobile.action(state, item.key) end
	end)
	context.connect(inputService.InputChanged, function(input)
		if not current or context.configuration.uis.lock[item.key] then return end
		if current.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input ~= current then return end
		if current.UserInputType ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then return end
		local delta = input.Position - origin
		if math.abs(delta.X) > 10 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 10 then moved = true end
		if not moved then return end
		local camera = context.workspace.CurrentCamera
		local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or environment.Vector2.new(800, 600)
		local x = math.clamp(start.X.Offset + delta.X, 0, math.max(0, viewport.X - button.AbsoluteSize.X))
		local y = math.clamp(start.Y.Offset + delta.Y, 0, math.max(0, viewport.Y - button.AbsoluteSize.Y))
		button.Position = environment.UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
		context.configuration.mobile.pos[item.key] = {x = x, y = y}
	end)
	context.connect(inputService.InputEnded, function(input)
		if not current then return end
		if current.UserInputType == environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input ~= current then return end
		if current.UserInputType ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input.UserInputType ~= environment.Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
		current = nil
		if moved then lastTime, lastPosition = nil, nil; return end
		if doubleTap then
			local locks = context.configuration.uis.lock
			locks[item.key] = not locks[item.key]
			lastTime, lastPosition = nil, nil
			PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state)
			return
		end
		lastTime, lastPosition = environment.os.clock(), environment.Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
		if item.key ~= "shot" then PulseApplication.Mobile.action(state, item.key) end
	end)
	return button
end

function PulseApplication.Mobile.paint(state)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local globals, settings = environment.getgenv(), context.configuration
	local active = {fly = settings.fly.on == true, bhop = settings.bhop == true}
	for key, name in {killall = "KillAll", invis = "Invis", noclip = "Noclip", speed = "Speed", sglitch = "SGlitch"} do
		local get = globals["__pulseGet" .. name]
		if type(get) ~= "function" then continue end
		local ok, value = pcall(get)
		active[key] = ok and value == true
	end
	local character = context.player.Character
	local backpack = context.player:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
	for _, item in PulseApplication.Mobile.buttons do
		local button = state.buttons[item.key]
		local unavailable = (item.key == "shot" or item.key == "knife") and not (
			character and character:FindFirstChild(item.key == "shot" and "Gun" or "Knife")
			or backpack and backpack:FindFirstChild(item.key == "shot" and "Gun" or "Knife"))
		local selected = active[item.key] or state.flinging == item.key
		local signature = tostring(selected) .. tostring(unavailable) .. tostring(settings.uis.style)
		if state.painted[item.key] == signature then continue end
		state.painted[item.key] = signature
		button.BackgroundColor3 = selected and environment.Color3.fromRGB(66, 135, 245)
			or settings.uis.style == "Glass" and environment.Color3.fromRGB(200, 205, 220) or environment.Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32)
		button.TextTransparency = unavailable and 0.55 or 0
	end
end

function PulseApplication.Mobile.hud(state)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local settings, section = context.configuration.mobile, context.section
	local globals = environment.getgenv()
	local function refresh()
		pcall(function()
			local window = globals.__pulseMM2Nova
			if not window then return end
			window:SetWatermark(settings.pingfps == true or settings.roundtimer == true)
			if not settings.roundtimer then window:SetWatermarkTitle(nil) end
		end)
	end
	local function setPing(value)
		settings.pingfps = value == true
		refresh()
		local control = globals.__pulsePfSettings
		if control and control.Get and control.SetSilent and control.Get() ~= settings.pingfps then pcall(control.SetSilent, settings.pingfps) end
	end

	section:Header("HUD")
	local control = section:Toggle({Text = "Show Ping/Fps UI", Default = false, Callback = setPing})
	globals.__pulsePingFps = {get = function() return settings.pingfps == true end, set = function(value)
		if control and control.Set then control.Set(value); return end
		setPing(value)
	end}
	section:Toggle({Text = "Show Round Timer", Default = false, Callback = function(value)
		settings.roundtimer = value == true
		refresh()
	end})
	setPing(settings.pingfps == true)

	local elapsed = 0
	context.connect(context.runService.Heartbeat, function(delta)
		if not settings.roundtimer then return end
		elapsed += delta
		if elapsed < 0.25 then return end
		elapsed = 0
		local title
		pcall(function()
			local part = context.workspace:FindFirstChild("RoundTimerPart")
			local time = part and part:GetAttribute("Time")
			if type(time) == "number" and time > 0 then title = string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(time / 60), math.floor(time % 60)) end
		end)
		local window = globals.__pulseMM2Nova
		if window and window.SetWatermarkTitle then pcall(window.SetWatermarkTitle, window, title) end
	end)
end

function PulseApplication.Mobile.install(context)
	local settings = context.configuration
	settings.mobile = type(settings.mobile) == "table" and settings.mobile or {}
	settings.mobile.pos = type(settings.mobile.pos) == "table" and settings.mobile.pos or {}
	settings.uis = type(settings.uis) == "table" and settings.uis or {}
	settings.uis.size = type(settings.uis.size) == "table" and settings.uis.size or {}
	settings.uis.lock = type(settings.uis.lock) == "table" and settings.uis.lock or {}
	if settings.uis.style ~= "Pulse" and settings.uis.style ~= "Glass" and settings.uis.style ~= "Compact" then settings.uis.style = "Pulse" end
	local state = {context = context, buttons = {}, painted = {}}
	local environment, section = context.environment, context.section
	state.gui = context.create("ScreenGui", {Name = "PulseMobileBtns", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true,
		ZIndexBehavior = environment.Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, DisplayOrder = 9999}, context.parent)
	section:Sub("UIS")
	section:Note({Text = "On-screen buttons (mobile). Hold & drag a button to move it, tap to use."})
	section:Header("Buttons Style")
	for _, item in PulseApplication.Mobile.buttons do state.buttons[item.key] = PulseApplication.Mobile.button(state, item) end
	section:Dropdown({Text = "Button Design", Default = settings.uis.style,
		Options = {{code = "Pulse", label = "Pulse"}, {code = "Glass", label = "Glass"}, {code = "Compact", label = "Compact"}}, Callback = function(value)
		settings.uis.style = value
		PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state)
	end})
	for _, item in PulseApplication.Mobile.buttons do
		section:Toggle({Text = item.text, Default = settings.mobile[item.key] == true, Callback = function(value)
			settings.mobile[item.key] = value
			PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state)
		end, Settings = function()
			section:Slider({Text = item.text .. " Size", Min = 50, Max = 200, Default = tonumber(settings.uis.size[item.key]) or 100,
				Decimals = 0, Suffix = "%", Callback = function(value)
					settings.uis.size[item.key] = value
					PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state)
				end})
		end})
	end
	section:Button({Text = "Reset UIS Position", Callback = function() table.clear(settings.mobile.pos); PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state) end})
	section:Header("Lock")
	section:Toggle({Text = "Double Tap Lock", Default = settings.uis.dtap == true, Callback = function(value) settings.uis.dtap = value end})
	section:Button({Text = "Unlock All Buttons", Callback = function() table.clear(settings.uis.lock); PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state) end})
	PulseApplication.Mobile.hud(state)
	local globals = environment.getgenv()
	globals.__pulseSyncUIS = function() PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state) end
	globals.__pulseRefreshBtnStyles = globals.__pulseSyncUIS
	table.insert(context.applyHooks, globals.__pulseSyncUIS)
	PulseApplication.Mobile.refresh(state)
	environment.task.spawn(function()
		while context.isAlive() do
			pcall(PulseApplication.Mobile.paint, state)
			environment.task.wait(0.25)
		end
	end)
	return state, function()
		state.cancelFling = true
		globals.__pulseSyncUIS, globals.__pulseRefreshBtnStyles, globals.__pulsePingFps = nil, nil, nil
		state.gui:Destroy()
	end
end

PulseApplication.Trolly = {}

function PulseApplication.Trolly.raycastParams(state, extra)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local params = environment.RaycastParams.new()
	local excluded = {}

	for _, player in ipairs(state.context.players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.Character then table.insert(excluded, player.Character) end
	end
	if state.model then table.insert(excluded, state.model) end
	if state.dance and state.dance.pig then table.insert(excluded, state.dance.pig.model) end
	local television = state.dance and state.dance.television
	if television then
		if television.model then table.insert(excluded, television.model) end
		if television.statue and television.statue.model then table.insert(excluded, television.statue.model) end
		if television.effectFolder then table.insert(excluded, television.effectFolder) end
	end
	local fight = state.dance and state.dance.fight
	if fight then
		if fight.statue and fight.statue.model then table.insert(excluded, fight.statue.model) end
		if fight.pig and fight.pig.model then table.insert(excluded, fight.pig.model) end
		if fight.effectFolder then table.insert(excluded, fight.effectFolder) end
	end
	if state.cut and state.cut.actor then table.insert(excluded, state.cut.actor) end
	if extra then table.insert(excluded, extra) end

	params.FilterType = environment.Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	params.FilterDescendantsInstances = excluded
	return params
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.horizontal(environment, vector)
	local horizontal = environment.Vector3.new(vector.X, 0, vector.Z)
	if horizontal.Magnitude < 0.001 then return nil end
	return horizontal.Unit
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.position(state)
	local context = state.context
	local environment, workspace = context.environment, context.workspace
	local regular = workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby")

	if regular then
		local board = regular:FindFirstChild("RadioBoard")
		local screen = board and board:FindFirstChild("Screen")
		if not screen or not screen:IsA("BasePart") then return nil end

		local forward = PulseApplication.Trolly.horizontal(environment, screen.CFrame.LookVector)
		local right = PulseApplication.Trolly.horizontal(environment, screen.CFrame.RightVector)
		if not forward or not right then return nil end
		return screen.Position - right * 18.2 - forward * 0.7, forward
	end

	local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
	local spawns = lobby and lobby:FindFirstChild("Spawns")
	if not spawns or not lobby:FindFirstChild("VotePads") then return nil end

	local ok, box = pcall(function() return spawns:GetBoundingBox() end)
	if not ok then return nil end

	local origin = box.Position
	local params = PulseApplication.Trolly.raycastParams(state, lobby:FindFirstChild("InvisibleWalls"))
	local wall = workspace:Raycast(origin + environment.Vector3.new(0, 18, 0), environment.Vector3.new(-120, 0, 0), params)
	if not wall or math.abs(wall.Normal.Y) > 0.2 then return nil end

	local normal = PulseApplication.Trolly.horizontal(environment, wall.Normal)
	if not normal then return nil end

	local floorOrigin = environment.Vector3.new(wall.Position.X, origin.Y + 4, wall.Position.Z) + normal * 4
	local floor = workspace:Raycast(floorOrigin, environment.Vector3.new(0, -20, 0), params)
	local base = environment.Vector3.new(wall.Position.X, floor and floor.Position.Y or origin.Y, wall.Position.Z)
	local tangent = environment.Vector3.yAxis:Cross(normal)
	return base + tangent * 7 + normal * 3.6, normal
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, className, properties, parent)
	local object = state.context.environment.Instance.new(className)
	for key, value in pairs(properties) do object[key] = value end
	object.Parent = parent
	return object
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.settings(state)
	local configuration = state.context.configuration
	if type(configuration.npc) ~= "table" then configuration.npc = {} end
	local settings = configuration.npc
	if settings.on == nil then settings.on = true end
	if settings.dances == nil then settings.dances = true end
	if tonumber(settings.volume) == nil then settings.volume = 100 end
	return settings
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.setOn(state, enabled)
	local settings = PulseApplication.Trolly.settings(state)
	settings.on = enabled and true or false
	if settings.on then
		pcall(PulseApplication.Trolly.ensure, state)
		return
	end
	pcall(PulseApplication.Trolly.remove, state)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.setVolume(state, value)
	local volume = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 100, 0, 100)
	PulseApplication.Trolly.settings(state).volume = volume
	pcall(function() PulseApplication.Trolly.soundGroup(state).Volume = volume / 100 end)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.soundGroup(state)
	if state.soundGroup and state.soundGroup.Parent then return state.soundGroup end

	local volume = tonumber(PulseApplication.Trolly.settings(state).volume) or 100
	state.soundGroup = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "SoundGroup", {
		Name = "PulseNpcSounds", Volume = math.clamp(volume / 100, 0, 1),
	}, state.context.soundService)
	return state.soundGroup
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.initVoices(state)
	if state.voices[1] and state.voices[1].Parent then return end

	state.voices = {}
	local group = PulseApplication.Trolly.soundGroup(state)
	for _, id in ipairs({9118480732, 9118480950, 9125850674}) do
		for _ = 1, 2 do
			local voice = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Sound", {
				Name = "PulseNpcVoice", SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. id, Volume = 0, SoundGroup = group,
			}, state.context.soundService)
			table.insert(state.voices, voice)
		end
	end
	state.voiceIndex = 0
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.syllable(state)
	PulseApplication.Trolly.initVoices(state)
	state.voiceIndex = state.voiceIndex % #state.voices + 1
	local voice = state.voices[state.voiceIndex]
	if voice.TimeLength <= 0.3 then return end

	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	voice:Stop()
	voice.PlaybackSpeed = 1.08 + math.random() * 0.28
	voice.TimePosition = math.random() * math.max(0, voice.TimeLength - 0.3)
	voice.Volume = 0.55
	voice:Play()
	environment.task.delay(0.085 + math.random() * 0.06, function()
		if not voice.Parent then return end
		context.tweenService:Create(voice, environment.TweenInfo.new(0.05), {Volume = 0}):Play()
		environment.task.delay(0.06, function()
			if voice.Parent and voice.Volume <= 0.01 then voice:Stop() end
		end)
	end)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.spooky(state, root)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "PointLight", {Name = "PulseGlow", Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 50),
		Brightness = 1.3, Range = 11, Shadows = false}, root)
	local mist = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Attachment", {Name = "PulseMist",
		Position = environment.Vector3.new(0, -2.6, 0)}, root)
	PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "ParticleEmitter", {Name = "Mist", Texture = "rbxassetid://101959242173269",
		Rate = 3, Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(3, 4.5), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0.4, 1),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(180, 10), Rotation = environment.NumberRange.new(0, 360),
		RotSpeed = environment.NumberRange.new(-15, 15), Size = environment.NumberSequence.new({
			environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2.5), environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 5)}),
		Transparency = environment.NumberSequence.new({environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
			environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0.75), environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}),
		Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(150, 90, 220), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 40)),
		LightEmission = 0.4, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0), ZOffset = -1}, mist)
	local embers = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Attachment", {Name = "PulseEmbers",
		Position = environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0)}, root)
	PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "ParticleEmitter", {Name = "Embers", Texture = "rbxassetid://16512006463",
		Rate = 4, Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(1.6, 2.6), Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(1, 2.2),
		SpreadAngle = environment.Vector2.new(60, 60), Drag = 1, Size = environment.NumberSequence.new({
			environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.18), environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)}),
		Transparency = environment.NumberSequence.new(0.1, 1), Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(
			environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 60), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 20)),
		LightEmission = 1, Acceleration = environment.Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)}, embers)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.loadAnimations(state, humanoid)
	local environment = state.context.environment
	local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or environment.Instance.new("Animator", humanoid)
	local ids = {idle = 507766388, look = 507766666, wave = 507770239, point = 507770453,
		cheer = 507770677, laugh = 507770818}

	for name, id in pairs(ids) do
		local animation = environment.Instance.new("Animation")
		animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		local ok, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(animation) end)
		animation:Destroy()
		if not ok then continue end

		track.Priority = (name == "idle" or name == "look") and environment.Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
			or environment.Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
		if name == "idle" then
			track.Priority = environment.Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
			track.Looped = true
			track:Play(0.3)
		end
		state.animations[name] = track
	end
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.gesture(state, name)
	local selected = state.animations[name]
	if not selected then return end

	for key, track in pairs(state.animations) do
		if key ~= "idle" and track.IsPlaying then track:Stop(0.25) end
	end
	selected.Looped = false
	selected:Play(0.2)
	state.context.environment.task.delay(name == "laugh" and 2.2 or 2.4, function()
		if selected.IsPlaying then selected:Stop(0.4) end
	end)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.build(state)
	local context = state.context
	local environment = context.environment
	local position, forward = PulseApplication.Trolly.position(state)
	if not position then return end

	local params = PulseApplication.Trolly.raycastParams(state)
	local ground = context.workspace:Raycast(position + environment.Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), environment.Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), params)
	if not ground then return end

	local description = environment.Instance.new("HumanoidDescription")
	local color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(236, 236, 236)
	for _, property in ipairs({"HeadColor", "TorsoColor", "LeftArmColor", "RightArmColor", "LeftLegColor", "RightLegColor"}) do
		description[property] = color
	end
	description.HatAccessory = "78557106937297,14926431610"
	description.BackAccessory, description.ShouldersAccessory, description.WaistAccessory = "110464967322599", "96098589382408", "82801950828150"
	description.Shirt, description.Pants = 5764463125, 18429601675

	local function createModel()
		return context.players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(description, environment.Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
	end
	local ok, model = pcall(createModel)
	if not ok or not model then
		description.HatAccessory, description.BackAccessory = "", ""
		description.ShouldersAccessory, description.WaistAccessory = "", ""
		description.Shirt, description.Pants = 0, 0
		ok, model = pcall(createModel)
	end
	description:Destroy()
	if not ok or not model then return end

	local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	local head = model:FindFirstChild("Head")
	if not humanoid or not root or not head then model:Destroy(); return end

	model.Name = "PulseNpc"
	humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = environment.Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
	humanoid.HealthDisplayType = environment.Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff
	for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendant.CanCollide, descendant.CanQuery, descendant.CanTouch = false, false, false
		end
	end
	root.Anchored = true

	local face, hats = nil, {}
	for _, accessory in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
		if not accessory:IsA("Accessory") or accessory.AccessoryType ~= environment.Enum.AccessoryType.Hat then continue end
		local handle = accessory:FindFirstChild("Handle")
		if not handle then continue end
		local size = handle.Size.X * handle.Size.Y * handle.Size.Z
		if not face or size > face.Size.X * face.Size.Y * face.Size.Z then
			if face then table.insert(hats, face) end
			face = handle
			continue
		end
		table.insert(hats, handle)
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("Decal") then child:Destroy() end
	end
	head.Transparency = 1

	if not face then
		face = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Part", {Name = "TrollHead", Size = environment.Vector3.new(2, 1, 1),
			Color = environment.Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Material = environment.Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
			CanCollide = false, CanQuery = false, CanTouch = false, Massless = true}, model)
		PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "SpecialMesh", {MeshType = environment.Enum.MeshType.Head,
			Scale = environment.Vector3.new(1.5, 1.5, 1.5)}, face)
		PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Decal", {Name = "face", Face = environment.Enum.NormalId.Front,
			Texture = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=9631028060&w=420&h=420"}, face)
		face.CFrame = head.CFrame * environment.CFrame.new(0, 0.22, 0)
		PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "WeldConstraint", {Part0 = face, Part1 = head}, face)
	end

	local headAttachment, faceAttachment = head:FindFirstChild("HatAttachment"), face:FindFirstChild("HatAttachment")
	for _, hat in ipairs(hats) do
		local attachment = hat:FindFirstChild("HatAttachment")
		if not headAttachment or not faceAttachment or not attachment then continue end
		local offset = headAttachment.CFrame * faceAttachment.CFrame:Inverse()
			* environment.CFrame.new(0, face.Size.Y / 2 + hat.Size.Y * 0.32, 0.18)
			* environment.CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(-8))
		attachment.CFrame = offset:Inverse() * headAttachment.CFrame
	end

	local height = ground.Position.Y + humanoid.HipHeight + root.Size.Y / 2
	local location = environment.Vector3.new(position.X, height, position.Z)
	state.home = environment.CFrame.lookAt(location, location + forward)
	model:PivotTo(state.home)
	model.Parent = context.workspace
	state.model, state.root, state.humanoid, state.neck, state.animations = model, root, humanoid, head:FindFirstChild("Neck"), {}
	pcall(PulseApplication.Trolly.spooky, state, root)
	PulseApplication.Trolly.loadAnimations(state, humanoid)
	state.lift = math.clamp(face.Position.Y + face.Size.Y / 2 - root.Position.Y, 1.6, 4) + 0.35
	PulseApplication.Trolly.createHud(state)
	PulseApplication.Trolly.createPrompt(state)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.lyricAt(lines, time, speaker)
	local selected, selectedIndex, best
	for index, line in ipairs(lines) do
		if line.who ~= speaker then continue end
		local start = line.words[1][1]
		if time < start - 0.3 or time > line.endsAt then continue end
		local priority = time >= start and start or start - 1000
		if not best or priority > best then selected, selectedIndex, best = line, index, priority end
	end
	if not selected then return nil end

	local active = 0
	for index, word in ipairs(selected.words) do if time >= word[1] then active = index end end
	local parts = {}
	for index, word in ipairs(selected.words) do
		local text = word[2]
		if index == active then text = '<font color="#FF4656">' .. text .. '</font>' end
		if index > active then text = '<font transparency="0.6">' .. text .. '</font>' end
		table.insert(parts, text)
	end
	local alpha = math.clamp((time - (selected.words[1][1] - 0.3)) / 0.2, 0, 1)
		* math.clamp((selected.endsAt - time) / 0.2, 0, 1)
	return table.concat(parts, " "), alpha, selectedIndex * 100 + active, active
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.lyricStep(state, time)
	local dance, label = state.dance, state.lyric
	if not label then return end
	if not dance or not dance.running or dance.ending or not dance.clip.lyrics then
		label.Visible = false
		state.lyricKey = nil
		return
	end
	local text, alpha, key, active = PulseApplication.Trolly.lyricAt(dance.clip.lyrics, time)
	if not text then label.Visible = false; state.lyricKey = nil; return end
	if key ~= state.lyricKey then
		state.lyricKey = key
		label.Text = text
		if active > 0 then state.lyricPop = os.clock() end
	end
	label.Visible = alpha > 0
	label.TextTransparency = 1 - alpha
	label.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - alpha * 0.65
	local elapsed = state.lyricPop and os.clock() - state.lyricPop or 1
	state.lyricScale.Scale = 1 + 0.14 * math.exp(-elapsed * 12)
end

PulseApplication.Trolly.danceJoints = {"Root", "Waist", "Neck", "RightShoulder", "RightElbow", "LeftShoulder", "LeftElbow",
	"RightHip", "RightKnee", "LeftHip", "LeftKnee"}

function PulseApplication.Trolly.statueLyricStep(state, time)
	local text, alpha, key, active = PulseApplication.Trolly.lyricAt(PulseApplication.Trolly.televisionLyrics, time, "s")
	if not text then state.statueLyric.Visible = false; state.statueLyricKey = nil; return false end
	if key ~= state.statueLyricKey then
		state.statueLyricKey = key
		state.statueLyric.Text = text
		if active > 0 then state.statueLyricPop = os.clock() end
	end
	state.statueLyric.Visible = alpha > 0
	state.statueLyric.TextTransparency = 1 - alpha
	state.statueLyric.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - alpha * 0.65
	local elapsed = state.statueLyricPop and os.clock() - state.statueLyricPop or 1
	state.statueWordScale.Scale = 1 + 0.14 * math.exp(-elapsed * 12)
	return alpha > 0
end

			{49.2,{leftLegX = -0.7, leftShoulderX = 2.6, leftShoulderZ = -1.4, neckX = 0.5, rightLegX = 0.7, rightShoulderX = 2.6, rightShoulderZ = 1.4, torsoPitch = 0.4}},

		statuePositions = {

			{17.9,{distance = 6.6, lateral = -0.3}},
			{23.25,{distance = 6.6, lateral = -0.3}},
			{23.56,{distance = 5.2, lateral = 0.1}},
			{24,{distance = 5.2, lateral = 0.1}},
			{24.299999999999997,{distance = 6.6, lateral = 0.2}},
			{33.3,{distance = 6.6, lateral = 0.2}},
			{33.95,{distance = 8.6, lateral = 0.6}},
			{34.1,{distance = 8.6, lateral = 0.6}},
			{34.4,{distance = 9, lateral = 0.5},"snap"},
			{35.5,{distance = 9, lateral = 0.5}},
			{36.1,{distance = 8.8}},
			{36.2,{distance = 8.8}},
			{36.32,{distance = 9.1},"snap"},
			{36.42,{distance = 9.1}},

			{37,{distance = 9.7}},
			{37.120000000000005,{distance = 10},"snap"},
			{37.620000000000005,{distance = 10}},

			{41.480000000000004,{distance = 11}},
			{42.14,{distance = 12.4},"lin"},
			{42.400000000000006,{distance = 12.4}},
			{42.480000000000004,{distance = 12.7},"snap"},
			{42.540000000000006,{distance = 12.7}},
			{42.620000000000005,{distance = 13},"snap"},
			{49.2,{distance = 13}},
		},
		statueWalk = {
			{12.2,13.95,1},
			{15.399999999999999,16.5,1},
			{17.4,17.9,1},
			{23.25,23.56,1},
			{24,24.299999999999997,1},
			{33.3,33.95,1},
			{35.5,36.1,1},
			{40.6,41.150000000000006,2},
		},
		knifeTrail = {
			{31.05,31.5},

			{41.050000000000004,41.300000000000004},
		},
		knifeTwirl = {
			{12.5,13.399999999999999,1},

		},
	}
	for _, frames in ipairs({timeline.trollyPoses, timeline.trollyPositions, timeline.statuePoses, timeline.statuePositions}) do
		PulseApplication.Trolly.normalizeTimeline(frames)
	end
	return timeline
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.normalizeTimeline(frames)
	local keys = {}
	for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
		for key in pairs(frame[2]) do keys[key] = true end
	end
	for _, frame in ipairs(frames) do
		for key in pairs(keys) do
			if frame[2][key] == nil then frame[2][key] = 0 end
		end
	end
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.timelineAt(frames, time)
	if time <= frames[1][1] then return table.clone(frames[1][2]) end
	for index = 1, #frames - 1 do
		local start, finish = frames[index], frames[index + 1]
		if time > finish[1] then continue end
		local progress = (time - start[1]) / math.max(finish[1] - start[1], 0.001)
		if finish[3] == "snap" then progress = 1 - (1 - progress) ^ 3 end
		if finish[3] ~= "snap" and finish[3] ~= "lin" then progress = PulseApplication.Trolly.danceSmooth(progress) end
		local result = {}
		for key, value in pairs(start[2]) do result[key] = value + (finish[2][key] - value) * progress end
		return result
	end
	return table.clone(frames[#frames][2])
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.danceWindow(time, first, last, before, after)
	before, after = before or 0.12, after or 0.15
	if time <= first - before or time >= last + after then return 0 end
	local progress = 1
	if time < first then progress = (time - first + before) / before end
	if time > last then progress = (last + after - time) / after end
	return progress * progress * (3 - 2 * progress)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.danceSmooth(value)
	value = math.clamp(value, 0, 1)
	return value * value * (3 - 2 * value)
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.blendPose(pose, weight, target)
	if weight <= 0 then return end
	for key, value in pairs(target) do pose[key] += (value - pose[key]) * weight end
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.poseTransforms(environment, pose)
	local cframe = environment.CFrame
	local dip = math.max(0, pose.dip)
	local knee = math.acos(math.clamp(1 - dip / 2.1, -1, 1))
	return {
		Root = cframe.new(pose.rootX, -dip + pose.hop, 0) * cframe.Angles(0, pose.rootYaw, pose.rootRoll),
		RightHip = cframe.Angles(knee + pose.rightStep, 0, 0),
		RightKnee = cframe.Angles(-2 * knee - pose.rightStep * 1.6, 0, 0),
		RightAnkle = cframe.Angles(knee + pose.rightStep * 0.4, 0, 0),
		LeftHip = cframe.Angles(knee + pose.leftStep, 0, 0),
		LeftKnee = cframe.Angles(-2 * knee - pose.leftStep * 1.6, 0, 0),
		LeftAnkle = cframe.Angles(knee + pose.leftStep * 0.4, 0, 0),
		Waist = cframe.Angles(pose.waistX, pose.waistY, pose.waistZ),
		Neck = cframe.Angles(pose.neckX, pose.neckY, pose.neckZ),
		RightShoulder = cframe.Angles(pose.rightShoulderX, 0, pose.rightShoulderZ),
		RightElbow = cframe.Angles(pose.rightElbowX, 0, pose.rightElbowZ),
		RightWrist = cframe.Angles(pose.rightWristX, 0, pose.rightWristZ or 0),
		LeftShoulder = cframe.Angles(pose.leftShoulderX, 0, pose.leftShoulderZ),
		LeftElbow = cframe.Angles(pose.leftElbowX, 0, pose.leftElbowZ),
		LeftWrist = cframe.Angles(pose.leftWristX, 0, pose.leftWristZ or 0),
	}
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.ugotPose(state, time)
	local trolly = PulseApplication.Trolly
	local period = 0.4838709677419355
	local beat = (time - 0.234) / period
	local beatIndex = math.floor(beat)
	local fraction = beat - beatIndex
	local pulse, swing = math.exp(-fraction * 7), math.sin(math.pi * beat)
	local intensity = (time < 3.45 and 0.5 or 1) * (time > 13.9 and 1 - 0.55 * trolly.danceSmooth((time - 13.9) / 1.2) or 1)
	local even = beatIndex % 2 == 0
	local pose = {
		rootX = 0.1 * swing * intensity, rootYaw = 0.22 * swing * intensity, rootRoll = 0.04 * swing * intensity,
		dip = (0.08 + 0.2 * intensity) * pulse + 0.05, hop = 0, rightStep = 0, leftStep = 0,
		waistX = -0.06 - 0.12 * pulse * intensity, waistY = 0.12 * swing * intensity, waistZ = -0.06 * swing * intensity,
		neckX = -0.25 * pulse * (0.5 + 0.6 * intensity), neckY = 0.14 * swing, neckZ = 0.06 * swing,
		rightShoulderX = 0.35 + 0.28 * swing * intensity, rightShoulderZ = 0.2,
		rightElbowX = 1.05 + 0.35 * pulse, rightElbowZ = 0, rightWristX = 0.2 * pulse,
		leftShoulderX = 0.35 - 0.28 * swing * intensity, leftShoulderZ = -0.2,
		leftElbowX = 1.05 + 0.35 * pulse, leftElbowZ = 0, leftWristX = 0.2 * pulse,
	}
	local window, blend = trolly.danceWindow, trolly.blendPose
	local function mix(first, last, target, before, after)
		blend(pose, window(time, first, last, before, after), target)
	end
	local point = math.max(window(time, 1.15, 1.95), window(time, 3.08, 3.85), window(time, 10.84, 11.12, 0.1, 0.1))
	blend(pose, point, {rightShoulderX = 1.5, rightShoulderZ = 0.12, rightElbowX = 0.08, rightWristX = 0,
		waistX = -0.18, neckX = -0.12, leftShoulderX = 0.15, leftShoulderZ = -0.15, leftElbowX = 1.6})

	local waving = window(time, 3.62, 5.4)
	if waving > 0 then
		local strength = math.exp(-fraction * 2.6)
		local right, left = even and strength or 0, even and 0 or strength
		blend(pose, waving, {rightShoulderX = 0.5 + 2.25 * right, rightShoulderZ = 0.25 + 0.1 * right,
			rightElbowX = 1.5 - 1.35 * right, leftShoulderX = 0.5 + 2.25 * left,
			leftShoulderZ = -0.25 - 0.1 * left, leftElbowX = 1.5 - 1.35 * left,
			leftStep = even and math.sin(math.pi * fraction) * 0.8 or 0,
			rightStep = even and 0 or math.sin(math.pi * fraction) * 0.8, neckX = -0.4 * pulse, waistX = -0.15 - 0.15 * pulse})
	end
	mix(5.42, 6.55, {rightShoulderX = 1.35, rightShoulderZ = 0.55, rightElbowX = 0.1,
		leftShoulderX = 1.35, leftShoulderZ = -0.55, leftElbowX = 0.1,
		waistX = 0.1 - 0.12 * pulse, neckX = 0.05 - 0.3 * pulse, rootYaw = 0.3 * swing})
	local crossed = math.max(window(time, 6.62, 7.92), window(time, 15.22, 15.7, 0.1, 0.05))
	blend(pose, crossed, {rightShoulderX = 0.55, rightShoulderZ = -0.15, rightElbowX = 1.95,
		leftShoulderX = 0.55, leftShoulderZ = 0.15, leftElbowX = 1.95, rightWristX = 0, leftWristX = 0,
		neckZ = 0.28 * math.sin(math.pi * beat * 0.5), neckY = 0.15 * swing, waistX = 0.05, waistZ = 0.12 * swing})
	local spin = time >= 7.98 and time <= 9.1 and 2 * math.pi * trolly.danceSmooth((time - 7.98) / 0.97) or 0
	mix(7.98, 8.95, {rightShoulderX = 0.15, rightShoulderZ = 1.45, rightElbowX = 0.15,
		leftShoulderX = 0.15, leftShoulderZ = -1.45, leftElbowX = 0.15, neckX = 0.15, waistX = 0.08, dip = 0.05, rootYaw = 0}, 0.08, 0.12)
	local reaching = window(time, 9.15, 10.6)
	if reaching > 0 then
		local strength = 0
		for _, moment in ipairs({9.22, 9.62, 9.94, 10.16}) do
			if time >= moment then strength = math.max(strength, math.exp(-(time - moment) * 9)) end
		end
		blend(pose, reaching, {rightShoulderX = 1.3, rightShoulderZ = 0.25, rightElbowX = 0.25 + 1.2 * strength,
			rightWristX = -0.5 * strength, leftShoulderX = 1.3, leftShoulderZ = -0.25,
			leftElbowX = 0.25 + 1.2 * strength, leftWristX = -0.5 * strength, waistX = 0.14, neckX = 0.1 - 0.15 * strength})
	end
	mix(11.12, 11.8, {rightShoulderX = 2.2 + 0.7 * pulse, rightShoulderZ = 0.4, rightElbowX = 0.2,
		leftShoulderX = 2.2 + 0.7 * pulse, leftShoulderZ = -0.4, leftElbowX = 0.2, neckX = 0.15 - 0.3 * pulse, hop = 0.25 * pulse})
	mix(11.85, 13.72, {rightShoulderX = (time - 11.85) / period * math.pi, rightShoulderZ = 0.18, rightElbowX = 0.05,
		leftShoulderX = 0.25, leftShoulderZ = -0.55, leftElbowX = 1.7, neckX = -0.5 * pulse,
		waistX = -0.28 * pulse - 0.1, dip = 0.12 + 0.25 * pulse, rootYaw = 0.15 * swing})
	mix(13.72, 14.3, {rightShoulderX = 0.1, rightShoulderZ = 2.65, rightElbowX = 0.05,
		leftShoulderX = 0.1, leftShoulderZ = -2.65, leftElbowX = 0.05, neckX = 0.25, neckY = 0, neckZ = 0,
		waistX = 0.15, waistY = 0, waistZ = 0, rootYaw = 0, rootRoll = 0, rootX = 0,
		hop = time >= 13.78 and 0.35 * math.exp(-(time - 13.78) * 5) or 0}, 0.08, 0.18)
	mix(14.3, 15.2, {rightShoulderX = 0.1, rightShoulderZ = 2.7, rightElbowX = 0.15,
		rootYaw = 0.1 * swing, rightElbowZ = 0.55 * math.sin(time * 13), leftShoulderX = 0.25,
		leftShoulderZ = -0.2, leftElbowX = 0.9, neckY = 0.1 * math.sin(time * 3)})
	return trolly.poseTransforms(state.context.environment, pose), spin
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.busikPose(state, dance, time)
	local trolly = PulseApplication.Trolly
	local beat = (time - 0.35) / 0.46153846153846156
	local beatIndex = math.floor(beat)
	local fraction = beat - beatIndex
	local pulse, swing = math.exp(-fraction * 7), math.sin(math.pi * beat * 0.5)
	local even = beatIndex % 2 == 0
	local lyricPulse = 0
	for _, line in ipairs(dance.clip.lyrics) do
		for _, word in ipairs(line.words) do
			local elapsed = time - word[1]
			if elapsed >= 0 and elapsed < 0.5 then lyricPulse = math.max(lyricPulse, math.exp(-elapsed * 9)) end
		end
	end
	local pose = {
		rootX = 0.06 * swing, rootYaw = 0.12 * swing, rootRoll = 0.03 * swing,
		dip = 0.05 + 0.08 * pulse, hop = 0, rightStep = 0, leftStep = 0,
		waistX = -0.05 - 0.05 * pulse, waistY = 0.1 * swing, waistZ = -0.04 * swing,
		neckX = -0.08 - 0.22 * lyricPulse, neckY = 0.1 * swing, neckZ = 0.06 * math.sin(time * 1.7),
		rightShoulderX = 0.55 + (even and 0.3 * pulse or 0), rightShoulderZ = 0.25,
		rightElbowX = 1.35 + (even and 0.3 * pulse or 0), rightElbowZ = 0, rightWristX = 0.2 * pulse, rightWristZ = 0,
		leftShoulderX = 0.55 + (even and 0 or 0.3 * pulse), leftShoulderZ = -0.25,
		leftElbowX = 1.35 + (even and 0 or 0.3 * pulse), leftElbowZ = 0, leftWristX = 0.2 * pulse, leftWristZ = 0,
	}
	local window, blend = trolly.danceWindow, trolly.blendPose
	local function mix(first, last, target, before, after)
		blend(pose, window(time, first, last, before, after), target)
	end
	local function mixWindows(windows, target)
		local weight = 0
		for _, interval in ipairs(windows) do
			weight = math.max(weight, window(time, interval[1], interval[2], interval[3], interval[4]))
		end
		blend(pose, weight, target)
	end
	local rightX, rightZ = dance.aimX or 1.5, dance.aimZ or 0.1
	mix(1.85, 2.2, {rightShoulderX = 1.1, rightShoulderZ = 0.35, rightElbowX = 0.9,
		leftShoulderX = 1.1, leftShoulderZ = -0.35, leftElbowX = 0.9, neckX = -0.1}, 0.1, 0.1)
	local gesture = window(time, 1.28, 1.85, 0.08, 0.12)
	if gesture > 0 then
		local progress = trolly.danceSmooth((time - 1.52) / 0.22)
		blend(pose, gesture, {rightShoulderX = 1.65 - 1.3 * progress, rightShoulderZ = 0.1,
			rightElbowX = 0.15 + 2.1 * progress, rightWristX = 0, waistX = -0.28 + 0.4 * progress,
			neckX = -0.2 + 0.3 * progress, dip = 0.12})
	end
	mixWindows({{2.2, 3.1}, {26.05, 26.9}}, {leftShoulderX = 0.25, leftShoulderZ = -1.45,
		leftElbowX = 0.05, leftWristX = 0, neckY = 0.5, neckX = 0, rightShoulderX = 0.3,
		rightShoulderZ = 0.45, rightElbowX = 1.6, waistY = 0.12})
	local waving = window(time, 3.36, 4.8)
	if waving > 0 then
		local movement = math.sin(time * 7)
		blend(pose, waving, {rightShoulderX = 1.25 + 0.3 * movement, leftShoulderX = 1.25 - 0.3 * movement,
			rightShoulderZ = -0.22, leftShoulderZ = 0.22, rightElbowX = 0.6, leftElbowX = 0.6,
			rootRoll = 0.08 * movement, waistZ = 0.1 * movement, neckX = 0, neckZ = 0.08 * movement})
	end
	local swaying = window(time, 4.85, 6.65)
	if swaying > 0 then
		local movement = math.sin(time * 2.5)
		blend(pose, swaying, {rightShoulderX = 0.1, rightShoulderZ = 1.5, rightElbowX = 0.05,
			leftShoulderX = 0.1, leftShoulderZ = -1.5, leftElbowX = 0.05, waistZ = 0.3 * movement,
			rootRoll = 0.1 * movement, rootYaw = 0.25 * math.sin(time * 1.6), neckZ = 0.15 * movement,
			rightStep = even and math.sin(math.pi * fraction) * 0.4 or 0,
			leftStep = even and 0 or math.sin(math.pi * fraction) * 0.4})
	end
	mix(6.7, 7.55, {rightShoulderX = 0.35, rightShoulderZ = 0.65, rightElbowX = 1.4,
		leftShoulderX = 0.35, leftShoulderZ = -0.65, leftElbowX = 1.4, neckZ = 0.2, waistX = 0.05})
	mixWindows({{7.6, 8.85}, {23.7, 24.42}}, {rightShoulderX = 0.55, rightShoulderZ = 1.15,
		rightElbowX = 2.1, rightWristX = 0.3, leftShoulderX = 0, leftShoulderZ = -0.08,
		leftElbowX = 0.05, leftWristX = 0, neckX = 0.12, neckY = 0, waistX = 0.06,
		rightStep = even and math.sin(math.pi * fraction) * 0.9 or 0,
		leftStep = even and 0 or math.sin(math.pi * fraction) * 0.9})
	local shaking = window(time, 9.55, 10.65)
	if shaking > 0 then
		local movement = math.sin(time * 12)
		blend(pose, shaking, {rightShoulderX = 1.25 + 0.45 * movement, leftShoulderX = 1.25 - 0.45 * movement,
			rightShoulderZ = -0.22, leftShoulderZ = 0.22, rightElbowX = 0.6, leftElbowX = 0.6,
			rootRoll = 0.15 * movement, waistZ = 0.18 * movement, neckZ = 0.2 * movement, neckY = 0.2 * math.sin(time * 9)})
	end
	mix(10.85, 11.3, {rightShoulderX = 0.4, rightShoulderZ = 2.2, rightElbowX = 0.1,
		leftShoulderX = 0.4, leftShoulderZ = -2.2, leftElbowX = 0.1, neckX = 0.35, waistX = 0.2,
		hop = time >= 10.88 and 0.3 * math.exp(-(time - 10.88) * 6) or 0}, 0.06, 0.12)
	mix(11.3, 12.4, {rightShoulderX = 2.5, rightShoulderZ = 0.45, rightElbowX = 2,
		leftShoulderX = 2.5, leftShoulderZ = -0.45, leftElbowX = 2, neckX = -0.25, neckY = 0.25 * math.sin(time * 10), waistX = -0.15})
	mix(12.6, 13.75, {rightShoulderX = 1.7, rightShoulderZ = 0.35, rightElbowX = 1.3, rightWristX = 0,
		rightWristZ = 0.55 * math.sin(time * 14), neckZ = -0.15, neckX = -0.05,
		leftShoulderX = 0.15, leftShoulderZ = -0.55, leftElbowX = 1.75})
	mix(15.85, 17, {rightShoulderX = -0.5, rightShoulderZ = 1.35, rightElbowX = 0.1, rightWristX = 0,
		neckY = -0.45, neckX = 0, waistY = -0.15, leftShoulderX = 0.15, leftShoulderZ = -0.55, leftElbowX = 1.75})
	mix(17.05, 18.45, {rightShoulderX = 0.1, rightShoulderZ = 1.4, rightElbowZ = 1.4, rightElbowX = 0,
		leftShoulderX = 0.1, leftShoulderZ = -1.4, leftElbowZ = -1.4, leftElbowX = 0,
		neckY = 0.3 * math.sin(time * 11), neckX = 0, waistX = 0.1, dip = 0.04})
	local lifting = window(time, 18.7, 20.15)
	if lifting > 0 then
		local progress = trolly.danceSmooth(fraction / 0.7)
		blend(pose, lifting, {rightShoulderX = 0.75 + 1.65 * progress, rightShoulderZ = 0.2,
			rightElbowX = 0.4 + progress, leftShoulderX = 0.9, leftShoulderZ = 0.15, leftElbowX = 1,
			waistX = -0.25, neckX = -0.2 - 0.15 * pulse, dip = 0.1 + 0.08 * pulse})
	end
	mix(20.3, 21.55, {neckY = 0.45 * math.sin(time * 3.2), rightShoulderX = 0.6, rightShoulderZ = -0.1,
		rightElbowX = 1.6, leftShoulderX = 0.6, leftShoulderZ = 0.1, leftElbowX = 1.6})
	mix(21.55, 23.1, {rightShoulderX = 1, rightShoulderZ = -0.25, rightElbowX = 1.5 + 0.9 * pulse,
		leftShoulderX = 0.8, leftShoulderZ = 0.2, leftElbowX = 1.3, neckX = -0.12 - 0.12 * pulse, waistX = -0.08})
	mix(23.3, 23.7, {rightShoulderX = 0.1, rightShoulderZ = 1.35, rightElbowZ = 1.6, rightElbowX = 0,
		leftShoulderX = 0.1, leftShoulderZ = -1.35, leftElbowZ = -1.6, leftElbowX = 0, neckX = 0.1}, 0.1, 0.1)
	mix(24.44, 26.05, {rightShoulderX = 0.95, rightShoulderZ = -0.75, rightElbowX = 1.85, rightWristX = 0,
		leftShoulderX = 0.95, leftShoulderZ = 0.75, leftElbowX = 1.85, leftWristX = 0, waistX = 0.08,
		neckY = time < 25.3 and 0.3 * math.sin(time * 9) or 0.05 * math.sin(time * 2), neckZ = 0.1})
	local alternate = window(time, 27.1, 28.7)
	if alternate > 0 then
		local progress = 1 - trolly.danceSmooth(fraction)
		blend(pose, alternate, {rightShoulderX = even and 1 + 0.9 * progress or 0.8,
			rightShoulderZ = 0.25, rightElbowX = 1.2, leftShoulderX = even and 0.8 or 1 + 0.9 * progress,
			leftShoulderZ = -0.25, leftElbowX = 1.2, neckX = 0.08, waistX = -0.05, dip = 0.08 + 0.1 * pulse})
	end
	mixWindows({{0, 1.25, 0.01, 0.12}, {8.9, 9.55}, {13.95, 15.2}, {28.7, 29.4}},
		{rightShoulderX = rightX, rightShoulderZ = rightZ, rightElbowX = 0.05, rightWristX = 0,
			rightWristZ = 0, rightElbowZ = 0, waistX = -0.12, leftShoulderX = 0.15,
			leftShoulderZ = -0.55, leftElbowX = 1.75, neckX = -0.05})
	return trolly.poseTransforms(state.context.environment, pose), 0
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.danceAim(state, dance, targetPosition)
	local shoulder = PulseApplication.Trolly.joints(state).RightShoulder
	if not shoulder or not shoulder:IsA("AnimationConstraint") or not shoulder.Attachment0 then return end
	local character = state.context.player.Character
	local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
	local target = targetPosition or head and head.Position
	if not target then return end

	local attachment = shoulder.Attachment0.WorldCFrame
	local direction = target - attachment.Position
	if direction.Magnitude < 1 or direction.Magnitude > 70 then return end
	local localDirection = attachment:VectorToObjectSpace(direction.Unit)
	local aimX = math.atan2(-localDirection.Z, -localDirection.Y)
	local aimZ = math.asin(math.clamp(localDirection.X, -1, 1))
	dance.aimX = dance.aimX and dance.aimX + (aimX - dance.aimX) * 0.25 or aimX
	dance.aimZ = dance.aimZ and dance.aimZ + (aimZ - dance.aimZ) * 0.25 or aimZ
end

PulseApplication.Trolly.statueParts = {"Torso", "Head", "Right Arm", "Left Arm", "Right Leg", "Left Leg"}

function PulseApplication.Trolly.statueWeapon(state, lobby, statue)
	local hand = statue:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
	local weapon
	for _, part in ipairs(state.context.workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(hand.Position, 3)) do
		if part:IsDescendantOf(statue) or not part:IsDescendantOf(lobby) then continue end
		local size = part.Size
		if math.max(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) >= 2 and math.min(size.X, size.Y, size.Z) <= 0.6 then weapon = part end
	end
	return weapon
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.statueFindNew(state, lobby)
	local closest, selected, weapon = 90, nil, nil
	for _, candidate in ipairs(lobby:GetDescendants()) do
		if not candidate:IsA("Model") or not candidate:FindFirstChild("Torso") or candidate:FindFirstChildOfClass("CharacterMesh") then continue end
		local valid = true
		for _, name in ipairs(PulseApplication.Trolly.statueParts) do
			local part = candidate:FindFirstChild(name)
			if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or not part.Anchored then valid = false; break end
		end
		if not valid then continue end
		local candidateWeapon = PulseApplication.Trolly.statueWeapon(state, lobby, candidate)
		if not candidateWeapon then continue end
		local distance = (candidate:FindFirstChild("Torso").Position - state.root.Position).Magnitude
		if distance < closest then closest, selected, weapon = distance, candidate, candidateWeapon end
	end
	if not selected then return nil end

	local extras = {}
	for _, part in ipairs(state.context.workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(selected:FindFirstChild("Head").Position, 2.2)) do
		if not part:IsDescendantOf(selected) and part ~= weapon and part:IsDescendantOf(lobby) and part.Size.Magnitude < 5 then
			table.insert(extras, part)
		end
	end
	return selected, weapon, extras
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.statueFind(state)
	if not state.root then return nil end
	local workspace = state.context.workspace
	local regular = workspace:FindFirstChild("RegularLobby")
	if not regular then
		local lobby = workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
		if not lobby then return nil end
		return PulseApplication.Trolly.statueFindNew(state, lobby)
	end
	local main = regular:FindFirstChild("MainLobby")
	local models = main and main:FindFirstChild("Models")
	if not models then return nil end

	local closest, selected = 90, nil
	for _, candidate in ipairs(models:GetChildren()) do
		if not candidate:IsA("Model") then continue end
		local valid = true
		for _, name in ipairs(PulseApplication.Trolly.statueParts) do
			if not candidate:FindFirstChild(name) then valid = false; break end
		end
		if not valid then continue end
		local distance = (candidate:FindFirstChild("Torso").Position - state.root.Position).Magnitude
		if distance < closest then closest, selected = distance, candidate end
	end
	if not selected then return nil end

	local weapon
	local parts = main:FindFirstChild("Parts")
	if parts then
		local hand = selected:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
		for _, part in ipairs(parts:GetChildren()) do
			if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == "Handle" and (part.Position - hand.Position).Magnitude < 4 then weapon = part end
		end
	end
	return selected, weapon
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.statueFree(statue)
	if not statue then return end
	PulseApplication.Trolly.statueRestore(statue)
	if statue.model then pcall(statue.model.Destroy, statue.model) end
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.statueClone(state, source, sourceWeapon, sourceExtras)
	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local ok, model = pcall(source.Clone, source)
	if not ok or not model then return nil end
	local statue = {model = model, parts = {}, offsets = {}, extras = {}, hidden = {}, eyes = {}}
	local built = pcall(function()
		for _, object in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") or object:IsA("Configuration") then object:Destroy(); continue end
			if object:IsA("BasePart") then object.CanCollide, object.CanQuery, object.CanTouch = false, false, false end
		end
		local humanoid = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if humanoid then
			pcall(function()
				humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = environment.Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None
				humanoid.HealthDisplayType = environment.Enum.HumanoidHealthDisplayType.AlwaysOff
			end)
		end
		model.Name = "PulseStatue"
		local sourceTorso = source:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		statue.originalFrame = sourceTorso.CFrame
		for _, name in ipairs(PulseApplication.Trolly.statueParts) do
			local part, original = model:FindFirstChild(name), source:FindFirstChild(name)
			assert(part and original)
			part.Anchored = true
			statue.parts[name] = part
			statue.offsets[name] = sourceTorso.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(original.CFrame)
		end

		if sourceWeapon then
			local weapon = sourceWeapon:Clone()
			for _, object in ipairs(weapon:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("JointInstance") or object:IsA("WeldConstraint") then object:Destroy() end
			end
			weapon.Anchored = true
			weapon.CanCollide, weapon.CanQuery, weapon.CanTouch = false, false, false
			weapon.Parent = model
			statue.weapon = weapon
			statue.weaponOffset = source:FindFirstChild("Right Arm").CFrame:ToObjectSpace(sourceWeapon.CFrame)
		end
		for _, original in ipairs(sourceExtras or {}) do
			local cloned, extra = pcall(original.Clone, original)
			if not cloned or not extra then continue end
			for _, object in ipairs(extra:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("JointInstance") or object:IsA("WeldConstraint") or object:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then object:Destroy() end
			end
			extra.Anchored = true
			extra.CanCollide, extra.CanQuery, extra.CanTouch = false, false, false
			extra.Parent = model
			table.insert(statue.extras, {part = extra, offset = source:FindFirstChild("Head").CFrame:ToObjectSpace(original.CFrame)})
		end
		local head = statue.parts.Head
		for _, side in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
			local attachment = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Attachment", {Position = environment.Vector3.new(side * 0.24, 0.12, -0.52)}, head)
			local eye = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "ParticleEmitter", {Texture = "rbxassetid://13484874938",
				Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40)), LightEmission = 1, Brightness = 6,
				Size = environment.NumberSequence.new(0.38), Lifetime = environment.NumberRange.new(0.1), Rate = 40,
				Speed = environment.NumberRange.new(0), LockedToPart = true, Enabled = false, Transparency = environment.NumberSequence.new({
					environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1), environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})}, attachment)
			table.insert(statue.eyes, eye)
		end
		statue.light = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "PointLight", {Color = environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 40),
			Brightness = 0, Range = 9}, head)
		local torso = statue.parts.Torso
		local top = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Attachment", {Position = environment.Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)}, torso)
		local bottom = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Attachment", {Position = environment.Vector3.new(0, -0.9, 0)}, torso)
		statue.trail = PulseApplication.Trolly.create(state, "Trail", {Attachment0 = top, Attachment1 = bottom,
			Lifetime = 0.38, LightEmission = 1, Brightness = 1.1, Enabled = false, FaceCamera = true,
			Color = environment.ColorSequence.new(environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 236, 200), environment.Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 60)),
			Transparency = environment.NumberSequence.new({environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5),
				environment.NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}), WidthScale = environment.NumberSequence.new(0.8, 0.05)}, torso)
		model.Parent = context.workspace

		local function hide(object)
			table.insert(statue.hidden, {object = object, transparency = object.LocalTransparencyModifier})
			object.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
		end
		for _, object in ipairs(source:GetDescendants()) do
			if object:IsA("BasePart") or object:IsA("Decal") then hide(object) end
		end
		if sourceWeapon then hide(sourceWeapon) end
		for _, extra in ipairs(sourceExtras or {}) do
			for _, object in ipairs(extra:GetDescendants()) do
				if object:IsA("BasePart") or object:IsA("Decal") then hide(object) end
			end
			hide(extra)
		end
	end)
	if not built then PulseApplication.Trolly.statueFree(statue); return nil end
	return statue
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.fightBuild(state)
	local trolly = PulseApplication.Trolly
	local source, weapon, extras = trolly.statueFind(state)
	if not source then return nil end

	local context, environment = state.context, state.context.environment
	local vector = environment.Vector3
	local home = state.home.Position
	local delta = source:FindFirstChild("Torso").Position - home
	local direction = vector.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
	if direction.Magnitude < 12 then return nil end
	direction = direction.Unit

	local statue = trolly.statueClone(state, source, weapon, extras)
	if not statue then return nil end
	local fight = {statue = statue, source = source, direction = direction, home = home,
		floor = home.Y - (state.humanoid.HipHeight + state.root.Size.Y / 2), fired = {}}
	local built = pcall(function()
		local params = trolly.raycastParams(state, statue.model)
		local origin = home + vector.new(0, 0.6, 0)
		local wall = context.workspace:Raycast(origin, -direction * 14, params)
		local distance = wall and (wall.Position - origin).Magnitude
		fight.retreat = home - direction * (distance and distance > 2.5 and distance - 1.05 or 6)
		fight.wall = distance and distance > 2.5 and wall.Position or nil
		fight.landing = home + direction * 8.5
		fight.clash = home + direction * 3
		fight.statueAttack = fight.clash + direction * 1.8
		fight.guideAttack = fight.statueAttack - direction * 2.7
		local sideways = direction:Cross(vector.yAxis).Unit
		local right = context.workspace:Raycast(fight.statueAttack, sideways * 14, params)
		local left = context.workspace:Raycast(fight.statueAttack, -sideways * 14, params)
		local rightDistance = right and (right.Position - fight.statueAttack).Magnitude or 14
		local leftDistance = left and (left.Position - fight.statueAttack).Magnitude or 14
		fight.side = sideways * (leftDistance > rightDistance and -1 or 1)
	end)
	if not built then trolly.statueFree(statue); return nil end
	pcall(trolly.televisionSound, state, fight, "crack", "torso", 1.2)
	return fight
end

function PulseApplication.Trolly.fightFree(state, fight)
	if not fight then return end
	PulseApplication.Trolly.statueFree(fight.statue)
	for _, name in ipairs({"pig", "effectFolder"}) do
		local object = fight[name]
		if name == "pig" and object then object = object.model end
		if object then pcall(object.Destroy, object) end
	end
	if fight.shakeBound then
		pcall(state.context.runService.UnbindFromRenderStep, state.context.runService, "PulseFightShake")
	end
end
